# Iggy's Blueprint — Research Appendix

## Feature Research (by domain)

### Daily Operations Cockpit
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] Shift Sessions — open/close the bar as a real object: A shift_sessions table + a big Dashboard 'Open the Bar / Close the Bar' control. Opening a shift starts a timestamped session (who is MOD, which spaces are live upstairs/downstairs, expected covers/weather), and the cockpit for the whole night hangs off it: every checklist tick, line-check reading, log note, drawer count, and the EON report is foreign-keyed to one shift. Closing requires the closing checklist + drawer reconciliation to be done, then generates the end-of-night report. This is the spine every other feature plugs into.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] Opening & Closing Checklists with photo proof + completion audit: Reusable checklist templates (Open, Close, plus space-specific upstairs/downstairs lists) with item types: simple tick, number entry, and 'photo required' (ice well stocked, garnish station, patio set, kegs tapped, bathrooms, floor swept). Each item logs who + exact time; photo items attach a Storage image. A live progress ring ('Closing 14/22') sits on the Dashboard, and a checklist can't be marked complete with required items skipped. Templates are editable in-app like MenuManager so the owner/Bradley tune them without a developer.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | M] Line Check with auto-fail temps + corrective-task escalation: A pre-service line/bar check: keg levels & line pressure, draft pour test, fridge/cooler temps, perishable garnish/dairy/citrus freshness, ice machine, CO2. Number fields carry a safe range; an out-of-range reading auto-fails, turns red, and auto-creates a corrective task ('cooler 2 reading 48°F — check seal / call refrigeration') assigned to the MOD with a deadline. If still open at close, it escalates into the owner's Dashboard and the EON report. Pass/fail history is charted per item so a slowly-warming cooler is visible before it spoils a Friday's stock.
- [P0 | wow4 util5 | M] Shift Log & Manager-on-Duty Journal: A structured, searchable per-shift log: quick-tag entries (86'd item, incident, comp/void, refused service, VIP, maintenance, staff late/no-show, weather event) plus free text and photos. 86 a menu item from here and it flags the matching MenuManager row as unavailable. Pass-down notes carry the unresolved threads to the next MOD ('downstairs AC making noise — watch it'). The whole log is filterable across dates so 'how many times did the ice machine fail this month' is one search, not a memory.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] Cash / Till Reconciliation with over-short tracking: Per-drawer flow: starting bank, denomination counter (tap-count twenties/tens/fives), expected total (manual entry now, POS-imported later), auto-computed variance + over/short, plus non-cash tender (card/tips) fields, deposit amount and a tip-pool split helper. Counts are signed (counter + MOD), large variances over a threshold prompt a required note before the shift can close, and over/short trends chart per drawer and per staffer so a recurring short is visible. Starts manual-entry-first since there's no POS integration yet, with a clean seam to pull 'expected' from Toast/Square later.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] End-of-Night Report — one tap, auto-composed, owner-delivered: At close, the cockpit assembles everything from the shift into one report: sales/covers (entered or POS-pulled), over/short, checklist completion %, line-check fails + open correctives, 86'd items, incidents, weather, notable log entries, and tomorrow's heads-up (parties, events, deliveries). Luna drafts the narrative summary on top of the structured numbers. Bradley reviews, edits, signs, and it's emailed to the owner (via the existing Gmail edge function) and archived as a browsable per-shift record — before he walks out.
- [P1 | wow4 util5 | S] Daily Run-Sheet & Pre-Shift Huddle card: An auto-built single screen for the day pulling from data the app ALREADY has: today's events (DJ/karaoke/trivia), confirmed parties + spaces + guest counts + service type, active specials/happy hour, low-stock and pending deliveries, plus weather and a covers/staffing projection. A 'Pre-Shift Huddle' summary (3-5 bullets the MOD reads to staff) generates from it, and Luna can voice/expand it. This is the morning glue between Events, Parties, Inventory, and Specials that currently live in separate silos.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | M] Task Assignment & Roles on the shift (beyond the Todos board): Lightweight per-shift assignment: attach checklist sections, line-check duties, and side-work to a named bartender/server/MOD for tonight, with a personal 'my tasks' view and tick-off. Distinct from the existing owner<->manager Todos board (strategic, dated) — this is operational, shift-scoped, and tied to who's actually working. Pulls names from the existing Team/manage-users data; recurring side-work (Monday deep-clean taps, Friday restock) auto-populates by day.
- [P1 | wow2 util5 | L] Offline-first cockpit (PWA) for the dead zones behind the bar: Make the operations cockpit work without signal: queue checklist ticks, temps, log entries, photos, and the drawer count locally (IndexedDB / service worker) and sync when the phone reconnects, with a clear 'pending sync' indicator. Install the manager app as a PWA on Bradley's phone so it launches like a native app and stays usable on the patio / in the cooler / during the inevitable seaside Wi-Fi drop.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | L] Luna as Operations Co-Pilot — photo review, EON drafting, exception alerts: Wire the home-lab bridge into the new ops tables: (a) read access to shift_sessions, checklists, line_checks, shift_log, cash_counts, eon_reports; (b) AI photo review — Luna looks at closing photos and flags 'patio chairs not stacked' as a note; (c) drafts the EON narrative and the pre-shift huddle bullets; (d) proactive insight cadences — a 'tonight' briefing pushed pre-shift, a real-time over/short alert if a drawer is short past threshold, a 'this is the 3rd cooler fail this week' pattern alert; (e) a durable Iggy's ops knowledge pack (par levels, normal over/short range, closing standards) so her judgments are calibrated.
- [P2 | wow4 util3 | S] Incident & Compliance log (alcohol service / safety): A structured incident sub-type of the shift log: refused service / overserve cut-off, ID checks/refusals, slips/injuries, breakages, comps/voids over a threshold, and equipment failures — each timestamped, photo-attachable, and routed into the EON report and a standalone incident archive. Optional witness/staff field and a 'reported to owner' flag.
- [P2 | wow5 util3 | M] Operations history & owner scorecard: A read-mostly analytics view over accumulated shift data: checklist completion % by week, over/short trend and total variance for the month, line-check fail frequency by station, most-86'd items, incident counts, and on-time open/close rates — with a shareable 'owner scorecard' the owner can pull anytime. Date-range filters and a simple sparkline-per-metric layout.

### Inventory Depth & COGS
- [P0 | wow3 util4 | M] Vendor Catalogs (suppliers as first-class entities): Promote the free-text inventory_items.supplier string to a real vendors table (contact, rep email/phone, order method, minimums, delivery days, account #) plus a vendor_catalog of purchasable products (pack size, case/each, vendor SKU, last price). Each inventory_item links to one or more vendor catalog lines. Backfill by de-duping the supplier strings already in the DB and the supplier names the Order Scanner has captured in the orders table.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Par-Based Auto-Reorder with Draft Purchase Orders: Extend the existing low-stock widget into a 'Build Order' flow: any item where current_quantity <= par_level (logic already in getLowStockItems) is bundled into draft POs grouped by vendor, with suggested order qty = (par - on_hand) rounded to the vendor's pack/case size. New purchase_orders + purchase_order_items tables hold status (draft/sent/received). One tap emails the PO to the rep via a new send-purchase-order edge function (mirrors the existing send-party-email/send-reply pattern), or copies it to the clipboard for vendors who only take texts.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | L] Pour & Recipe Costing tied to the live menu: A recipes table mapping each menu item (cocktails, on_tap, food via the existing menu tables) to its inventory ingredients with exact yields (1.5 oz well vodka, 0.75 oz lime, etc.) and a bottle-yield config (750ml = 16.9 oz, keg = 124 pints). Auto-compute cost-per-drink and pour cost % = ingredient cost / menu price, surfaced inline in MenuManager. Flag any cocktail whose pour cost drifts above a target band (e.g., >24%) when a linked ingredient's cost_per_unit rises.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] COGS% & Theoretical-vs-Actual Variance Report: A period report (week/month) computing actual usage from inventory_logs (the typed restock/usage/waste/count entries already captured) valued at cost_per_unit, vs. theoretical usage derived from the recipe map x units sold. Output: COGS$ and COGS% by category (Liquor/Beer/Wine/Food), variance $ and %, and a ranked 'biggest leaks' list. Period boundaries anchor to dated stocktake snapshots (next feature). Target bands shown inline: 18-24% pour cost, with red flag above acceptable variance.
- [P1 | wow4 util5 | L] Full Stocktake Sessions (count-by-area, mobile, fast): A stocktake_sessions + stocktake_counts model so a count is a single dated event rather than scattered 'count' log rows. Mobile flow: pick an area (Back Bar / Well / Walk-in / Upstairs store), walk it, enter counts (whole + tenths-of-a-bottle, e.g. 3.4), with optional Bluetooth scale weight-to-volume entry. On close: write a valuation snapshot ($ on hand by category), auto-generate inventory_logs 'count' adjustments for any deltas (reusing adjustQuantity), and stamp the period boundary the COGS report consumes.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | M] Supplier Price-Change Tracking (off the invoice scanner): On every confirmed Order Scan, diff each matched line's unit price against the last recorded price for that vendor+product and write a price_history row. Show a sparkline + 'last paid' on the item, and raise a luna_insight / low-key alert when a price jumps beyond a threshold (e.g. +5%). Roll affected drinks' pour cost forward automatically so margin flags stay honest.
- [P1 | wow3 util4 | M] Waste & Spillage Log with reasons + cost roll-up: Upgrade the existing 'waste' log reason into a quick-capture waste flow: tap item, qty, reason (spill/breakage/expired/comp/86'd/training), optional photo to Supabase Storage. A waste dashboard totals $ wasted by reason and category per period and feeds the variance report's explanation column so 'unexplained variance' shrinks to the real shrinkage.
- [P2 | wow4 util3 | L] Seasonal / Day-of-Week Smart Par Levels: Track usage velocity from inventory_logs and let pars flex by season and weekday — a 'suggested par' the manager accepts or overrides, e.g. summer Friday well tequila par auto-rises vs. a quiet February Tuesday. Seaside-specific: tie par suggestions to the events calendar (a booked DJ Night / Karaoke / private party upstairs lifts the relevant pars for that week).
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | M] Luna 'COGS Watchdog' proactive insight cadence: Give the home-lab Luna bridge read access to the new tables (variance, price_history, waste, stocktake snapshots, draft POs) plus a knowledge-pack of Iggy's targets (pour-cost bands, vendor terms, season). Luna writes scheduled luna_insights: a Monday 'last week COGS was 22.4% (+1.8 over target), the leak is well vodka — 11% variance 3 weeks running'; a 'Coastal Distributors raised tequila 9%, here are 3 cocktails now over 24% pour cost, want me to draft a price bump?'; and pre-order nudges before a busy event week. She can draft the PO email or a menu-price-change for one-tap approval.

### Staff Scheduling & Labor
- [P0 | wow3 util5 | M] Roster + SMS portal: Real staff with availability, time-off, open-shifts, one-tap swaps, chat. Team today only manages logins.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Schedule + live labor-percent gauge: Drop shifts; gauge shows labor vs forecast sales; cert/OT staff blocked.
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | L] Demand forecast: Day-part sales from history, weather, booked parties; pre-fills staffing.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Tip pooling + SPLH/cert alarms: Pool by hours/role points, same-day payout per SB 648; SPLH and cert alarms.

### Private Events & BEOs
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] One-page BEO document (generate from the party record): A 'Generate BEO' button on PartyProfile that renders the existing party + party_packages data into a structured, print/PDF one-page Banquet Event Order: header (event title, date, contact, guest count, space, private/general), a setup block (room layout note, setup_time, arrival/start/end), the menu/service section (food_service_type, package line items, food/drink notes), beverage plan, special_requests, and a staffing/notes footer. Reuses the buildInvoiceHtml pattern in utils/invoice.ts; adds a beo_documents row (version, status draft/final, pdf_url in Storage) so each revision is captured. A 'BEO version' badge and a T-30/T-14/T-7/T-2 sign-off checklist (event/kitchen/FOH acknowledged) sit on the profile.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | L] Shareable client proposal + portal link (no login): A public, tokenized read-only page (Netlify route /p/:token backed by a proposals row + Supabase RLS on the token) that shows the branded proposal: event summary, the itemized package/price breakdown straight from computeInvoice(), an 'Accept proposal' button, and (once wired) deposit pay + e-sign. A 'Send proposal' action on PartyProfile generates the link and emails it via the existing send-party-email function. Status tracking: sent / viewed / accepted (a viewed_at timestamp via an edge function the page pings), surfaced as a badge next to the party.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | L] Deposit collection + payment schedule (wire Stripe for parties): Wire the installed-but-stubbed Stripe into the proposal/portal: a create-deposit-checkout edge function creates a Stripe Checkout Session for a configurable deposit (flat or % of grand total), and a stripe-webhook function records payment to a new party_payments table (amount, type deposit/balance, status, stripe_id). PartyProfile gains a 'Payments' card showing deposit due / paid / balance due with a status pill, and a payment SCHEDULE (e.g. deposit on booking, balance T-7) with automated reminder emails. Balance-due and overdue states feed the dashboard.
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | L] E-signature contracts on the proposal: Add a contract layer to the proposal/portal: a contracts table (party_id, template, rendered_html, signer_name, signed_at, signature_image/typed_name, signed_ip, status sent/viewed/signed) plus a reusable contract template (terms, cancellation policy, deposit terms) filled from party fields via the existing fillTemplate util. The client types/draws a signature on the portal page; a sign-contract edge function captures it with timestamp + IP and writes the signed PDF to Storage. PartyProfile shows signature status and a 'Download signed contract' link; signing can auto-advance the party to confirmed.
- [P1 | wow4 util5 | M] Run-of-show timeline builder: A timeline section on PartyProfile: an ordered list of timed cues (party_timeline rows: time/offset, who FOH/kitchen/bar/DJ, action, notes) — e.g. 5:00 doors, 5:15 apps out, 6:00 toast, 8:00 last call, 9:00 breakdown. Seeded from the chosen party preset (birthday/corporate/celebration-of-life/holiday already exist in partyPresets.ts) so it starts 80% filled. Renders into the BEO and into a phone-friendly 'day-of view' Bradley can run from behind the bar, with the next cue highlighted.
- [P1 | wow5 util3 | M] Online-bookable packages on the public site (BookEvent): Surface the existing packages catalog (food/drink/room/addon, flat/per_person/per_hour) on the public BookEvent page as branded, priced cards with a live estimate calculator (pick package + guest count + hours → instant total using the same computeInvoice logic). 'Request this package' pre-fills the booking request via the existing submit-booking-request function so the party lands with packages already attached. Adds active/featured + a public_description field to packages (catalog already has active + sort_order).
- [P2 | wow5 util3 | XL] Visual floor plan / room layout per event: A lightweight 2D layout editor for the upstairs + downstairs spaces: pre-drawn room shells (Storage SVG/image background) with drag-and-drop table/bar/DJ/dancefloor tokens snapped to a grid, saved per party as a layout JSON + a flattened PNG (party_layouts row). The layout image embeds in the BEO and can be shared on the client proposal. Start with reusable room TEMPLATES (e.g. 'Upstairs cocktail', 'Upstairs seated 40') so most events pick a template and tweak.
- [P2 | wow3 util4 | M] BEO change-log + version diff with re-acknowledge: Track material changes to a confirmed party (guest_count, menu, timeline, space, totals) as beo_change rows; when a change crosses a threshold after the BEO is final, flag it as a 'change order' that resets the kitchen/FOH/event sign-offs to pending and can fire an updated-BEO email. PartyProfile shows a compact change history ('guest count 30 → 45, +$340, by Bradley, 2 days out').
- [P3 | wow3 util4 | S] Tasting & site-visit scheduling tied to the party: A small scheduling block on PartyProfile for pre-event touchpoints (tasting, site visit, final-details call): pick a slot, write a calendar event via the existing google-calendar function, and send a confirmation via send-party-email. These appear on the unified Calendar alongside the event and feed the follow-up cadence.

### Social Media & Content Marketing
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | XL] True multi-platform auto-publish (the owner's #4 ask): Replace the manual navigator.share sheet in SpecialEditor with real API publishing. New social_posts queue table (status: draft/scheduled/publishing/posted/failed, target platforms array, asset_url, per-platform caption JSON, scheduled_at, external_post_ids). A new social-publish edge function holds the OAuth tokens and does the actual posting: Instagram Graph API (POST /media container then /media_publish for feed/Reels/Stories/carousels), Facebook Page API, TikTok Content Posting API, and Google Business Profile posts. Manager hits one 'Publish' (or 'Schedule') button; picks platforms via toggle chips; the existing 1080x1080/GIF/MP4 export feeds straight in. Status + permalink written back to the row and shown in the app.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Content Calendar that auto-drafts from Iggy's own data: A new Calendar-style view (week/month) of all scheduled + posted social. The differentiator: a 'Fill my week' button that reads the events, specials, happy_hour, and parties tables and auto-drafts a ready-to-approve post queue — e.g. Thursday 11am GBP+IG 'Trivia tonight 8pm' pulled from the recurring event; Friday 4pm 'Happy Hour 4-7' from happy_hour rows; Saturday 'DJ Night' from events. Drag to reschedule, color-coded by platform, gaps highlighted ('no post Tuesday'). Each draft is one tap from the auto-publish pipeline above.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] Luna AI caption + hashtag + best-time engine (bridge-native): Every draft gets a 'Draft with Luna' affordance. The app writes a luna_messages/social-draft row with the special/event context; home-lab Luna (with the Iggy's knowledge pack + brand voice) writes back a per-platform caption set: punchy IG/TikTok hook, fuller Facebook, GBP with a hard CTA ('Click to book' not 'link in bio'), plus a curated hashtag block (local + nightlife + seasonal seaside tags) and a recommended post time from past engagement. Manager edits inline or accepts. Brand-voice guardrails live in Luna's knowledge pack, not a generic GPT prompt.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | L] UGC inbox + one-tap repost-permission flow: A 'Tagged' tab that pulls @mentions and tagged photos/Reels of Iggy's via the IG/FB Graph API mentions endpoints. Each item shows the customer post; manager taps 'Request to repost' which fires a templated, friendly permission DM/comment ('Love this! Can we feature it? Reply YES'); on YES the asset drops into a 'Social Bank' folder in MediaLibrary tagged ugc=true with rights-granted timestamp. From there it's one tap into a Story or feed post. A 'Tag @iggys to get featured' QR card generator (table tents) seeds the funnel.
- [P1 | wow4 util3 | M] Live link-in-bio page (powered by the real site DB): A public /l/iggys micro-page (new lightweight route or Netlify page) that is the link-in-bio destination for all platforms. Unlike a dead Linktree, it renders LIVE from Supabase: tonight's event, this week's happy hour, the menu link, 'Book your party' (deep-link into BookEvent + the online package booking), the shop, reservations, directions. Auto-reorders by relevance (event tonight floats to top). One toggle in the manager app sets the 'featured' button. UTM-tagged so clicks show up in analytics.
- [P2 | wow3 util4 | M] Story template pack tied to today's event: Extend the existing SpecialEditor template system with a Stories-specific set sized 1080x1920: 'Tonight' countdown, 'Happy Hour now', 'We're open', 'Last call', poll/question stickers, 'Tag us' frame, weather-aware 'Perfect patio night'. Templates auto-fill from the events/happy_hour tables (tonight's DJ name, the time, the special price) so a full Story is one tap. Schedulable through the same auto-publish queue (IG/FB Stories via Graph API).
- [P2 | wow4 util3 | L] Post performance dashboard + Luna weekly insight cadence: A simple analytics card per post (reach, likes, saves, GBP views/clicks/calls/direction-requests) pulled from the platform insights APIs into a social_post_metrics table. Roll-up tile on the manager Dashboard: 'This week: 6 posts, 4.2k reach, Trivia Reel was your top post.' Home-lab Luna reads social_post_metrics on a schedule and pushes a luna_insights row every Monday: what worked, what to repeat, which night is underposted, suggested next-week themes — proactively, before Bradley asks.
- [P3 | wow2 util3 | M] Approval/guard rail + scheduled-queue safety: Lightweight publish-guard: drafts can be marked 'needs owner approval' (surfaces on the owner<->manager Todos board), token-expiry warnings ('Reconnect Instagram'), a 'pause all scheduled' kill switch, and a failed-post retry with clear error ('TikTok rejected: video too short'). Plus a small media-rights check so a UGC asset can't be auto-published without the rights-granted flag set.

### Website CMS, SEO & Local SEO
- [P0 | wow4 util4 | M] Schema Auto-Sync: Menu, Event & FAQ structured data generated from Supabase: An edge function (build-time + on-write trigger) that reads the existing menu tables (appetizers, on_tap, off_tap, cocktails, shots, happy_hour, menu_categories/items/options) and events table and emits valid schema.org JSON-LD — Restaurant+Menu+MenuItem with prices, Event for every upcoming public event (name, startDate, location, offers), and a FAQPage block (hours, parking, dog policy, private-event capacity). Injected into the prerendered HTML per route, NOT client-side. A 'Schema' tab in the manager app shows each page's live structured data, a one-click Rich Results validity check, and a 'last pushed' timestamp.
- [P0 | wow3 util4 | L] Per-route SEO via prerender (fix the one-head-for-every-page SPA problem): Add vite-react-ssg (or a prerender step) so each public route ships its own server-rendered HTML with a unique <title>, meta description, canonical, OG image, and route-specific JSON-LD — instead of today's single static index.html head reused for all 11 routes. Pair with a lightweight 'Page SEO' editor in the manager app: for each page, Bradley sees/edits the title, meta description, OG image, and a live Google/social preview, with character-count guidance and a Luna 'suggest' button.
- [P0 | wow4 util3 | M] Core Web Vitals rescue: responsive WebP/AVIF pipeline + CWV health panel: Convert the 5-6MB PNG heroes/features (hero-bar 5.2MB, seafood 5.8MB, cocktails 5.7MB, beers 5.5MB) to responsive AVIF/WebP with width-based srcset, explicit width/height (no layout shift), lazy-loading below the fold, and a preloaded LCP hero. Add an automatic Supabase Storage transform so any image the manager uploads to MediaLibrary is auto-resized/compressed. Surface a 'Site Health' card in the manager dashboard: live LCP/INP/CLS from CrUX/PageSpeed API per page, with red/yellow/green and 'what to fix' plain-English tips.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | L] Google Business Profile cockpit inside the manager app: Integrate the Google Business Profile API (Bradley already has Google OAuth via the Calendar/Gmail edge functions) into a manager-app panel: see and reply to reviews, read profile Insights (search views, direction requests, calls, photo views, website clicks), post GBP 'Posts' (events/offers) straight from the Specials/Events the team already creates, update hours (incl. holiday hours) once and have them sync to GBP, and a NAP-consistency checker that flags if the GBP address/phone differs from the site schema. Review replies and GBP posts can be Luna-drafted.
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | L] Luna AI-Search & Citation Monitor (AEO/GEO for 'Seaside, Oregon'): A Luna insight cadence that periodically asks the AI answer engines real customer questions ('best seaside bar in Seaside Oregon', 'live music Seaside tonight', 'dog-friendly bar Oregon coast', 'happy hour near the Promenade') and reports whether Iggy's is named/cited, what it's beating or losing to, and which facts the AI got wrong or stale. When it finds a gap, Luna drafts the fix — a new FAQ entry, a schema field, a GBP post, or an About-page line — and drops it as a one-tap-to-apply suggestion. Also runs a quarterly entity/NAP consistency audit across the site, GBP, and major directories.
- [P2 | wow3 util4 | M] Indexing & sitemap automation + Search Console health feed: Auto-generate sitemap.xml from live routes + DB content (so new events/menu pages and accurate lastmod dates appear automatically, replacing the hand-maintained static sitemap), ping Google on publish, and pull Google Search Console data into the dashboard: indexed-vs-submitted count, top queries/clicks/impressions, coverage errors, and per-page index status with a one-tap 'Request indexing'. Also fix the live host inconsistency (robots.txt points to www.iggysseaside.com while canonical/OG use the non-www apex) and add hreflang/clean canonicals per route.
- [P2 | wow3 util3 | M] Conversion & local-intent landing pages (book/menu CRO): Generate SEO landing pages that match high-intent local searches the bar should own — 'private events Seaside Oregon', 'oceanfront bar Oregon coast', 'live music tonight' — each prerendered with Event/FAQ schema, a sticky 'Book / Get Directions / Call' bar, and a clear above-the-fold CTA. Add CRO instrumentation the manager can read: which pages drive booking-form starts vs completes, CTA click-through, and an A/B toggle on the BookEvent hero. Pair with a 'pin to Google Maps directions' deep link and click-to-call that's prominent on mobile.
- [P3 | wow2 util3 | M] Accessibility (a11y) pass for SEO + ADA compliance: Automated + manual a11y remediation surfaced as a manager-app checklist: alt text on every image (auto-prompt when uploading to MediaLibrary, Luna can draft descriptive alt from the filename/context), color-contrast fixes on the dark seaside theme, focus states and keyboard nav, proper heading hierarchy and landmarks, accessible form labels on Contact/BookEvent, and a 'skip to content' link. A periodic axe-core scan reports a score and the top issues in plain language.

### Reservations, Waitlist & Tables
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Virtual text-to-join waitlist (host stand on Bradley's phone): A new manager-app screen and a public QR/web link that lets walk-ins add themselves (name, party size, phone) or lets Bradley add them in two taps. Live list with quoted wait, color-coded by how long they've waited, and a one-tap 'Notify — table ready' that fires an SMS. Two-way SMS so guests can reply 'HERE', 'OMW', or 'CANCEL', which moves them on the list automatically. Public-facing 'you're #4, ~25 min' status page via the same link.
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | XL] Drag-and-drop floor plan with sections, turn timers & door pacing: A visual map of Iggy's real room — bar rail, hi-tops, patio/deck, upstairs, downstairs — with tappable tables that show status (open / seated / check dropped / needs bussing) and a live turn timer per table. Host drags a waitlist or reservation chip onto a table to seat it. A 'pacing' guardrail warns when too many parties land in the same 15-min window so the kitchen and single bar don't get buried. Sections can be opened/closed as staffing changes through the night.
- [P0 | wow4 util4 | XL] Timed online table reservations (public site + manager calendar): A net-new public reservation widget (separate from the private-EVENT BookEvent form) where guests pick a date, party size and time SLOT against real per-slot capacity, not whole-day blocking. Confirmation + reminder via email (Gmail edge fn already exists) and SMS. In the manager app these flow into the floor plan and a day/agenda view alongside events and parties. Capacity rules per section and per slot so the room can't be oversold.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | L] Deposits & credit-card holds on large groups / package bookings (Stripe): Wire the already-installed Stripe (today a stub) into a deposit/hold flow on the BookEvent large-group + package path and on big-top reservations. Owner sets per-booking rules (e.g. flat $200 deposit, or $25/head card hold for groups of 8+, applied to the existing packages catalog). A new Supabase edge function creates the Checkout Session / SetupIntent; the deposit/hold state shows on the Party and reservation record; auto-charge or release on no-show per policy.
- [P1 | wow3 util4 | M] No-show & cancellation handling with policy + history: Add a 'no_show' status (and late-cancel) to reservations and to the parties model (which today only has inquiry/confirmed/cancelled). One-tap mark-no-show that, per policy, captures the deposit / charges the held card and stamps the guest profile. A no-show/late-cancel counter per guest, a cancellation-policy line shown at booking and in reminders, and a daily 'who didn't show' rollup.

Phase 1 (ship now): fast manual logging — pick a unit (walk-in, beer cooler, reach-in, freezer, hot-hold), the app shows the safe range, you tap the reading, done; out-of-range readings force a 'corrective action' note (moved stock / called tech / discarded). Phase 2 (the wow): integrate $40-60 WiFi/Bluetooth sensors (Monnit/MOCREO/SensMax) via a tiny edge-function webhook that writes readings into a temperature_logs table every few minutes and pushes a Luna alert the instant a walk-in drifts out of range — overnight, before a cooler full of seafood spoils. New tables: temp_units, temperature_logs.
- [P0 | wow4 util5 | M] Opening / Closing & Safety Checklists (with photo proof): Recurring, role-aware checklists that staff run on their phone: Opening (unlock, lights, fridge temps logged, floors dry, POS up, first-aid stocked, exits clear), Closing (lock liquor, gas/equipment off, deposits secured, alarms set, last floor-check photo), and a Weekly Safety sweep (fire extinguisher tags, exit signs, deck railings/decking for a seaside venue, GFI/electrical near water). Items can require a photo or a number (e.g. a temp). Each completed checklist is timestamped and signed by the staff member; misses are flagged on the dashboard and surfaced to Luna. Builds on the existing Todos pattern but as recurring templated runs. New tables: checklist_templates, checklist_items, checklist_runs, checklist_responses.
- [P0 | wow5 util4 | S] License / Permit / Insurance / Certification Tracker: A single 'Credentials' page tracking every dated document the bar lives or dies by: liquor license, business license, food-service permit, music/PRS license, the certificate of insurance (COI), fire inspection, plus per-staff RBS/alcohol-server certs and food-handler cards. Each record stores issuer, number, issue/expiry date, a photo/PDF of the document in Supabase Storage, and a renewal lead-time. Luna pushes escalating insight alerts at 90/60/30/7 days out (60-90 days is the real renewal-application window). A red banner appears if anything is expired. Staff-cert view shows who is current vs. lapsed.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | L] Cleaning & Preventive Maintenance Scheduler (QR assets + work orders): A recurring task engine for cleaning (beer-line clean every 2 weeks, glasswasher descale, deep-clean rota, restroom checks) AND preventive maintenance of equipment assets (walk-in, ice machine, glasswasher, HVAC filters monthly, deck/railing/exterior salt-air corrosion checks quarterly). Each asset gets a record and an optional printable QR sticker — scan it on the unit to see history and log service. When a check fails or something breaks, raise a Work Order with a priority (Critical/Standard/Scheduled), photo, and assignee; the owner sees open work orders on a board. New tables: assets, maintenance_schedules, work_orders.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | S] Compliance Dashboard Score + Inspector-Ready Mode: A single dashboard tile that rolls everything up into a live Compliance Health score and a traffic-light strip: temps logged today (Y/N), opening checklist done, any license inside its alert window, open critical work orders, incidents this week. Tapping it opens 'Inspector Mode' — a clean, read-only, full-screen view that surfaces the temperature wall, today's checklists, current licenses/COI, and the last cleaning records, designed to be handed across the bar to a health or alcohol-board inspector. One 'Export Compliance Pack (last 30/90 days)' button produces a single PDF binder for the owner, insurer, or franchise.
- [P2 | wow3 util3 | S] Allergen & Recall Register: Pull the existing menu_items / cocktails / food tables into a lightweight allergen matrix (the 14 major allergens + 'contains shellfish/nuts' flags), maintained per item, so any staffer can answer 'does this have nuts?' in two taps behind the bar. Add a supplier-recall log: when a recall hits (e.g. a shellfish or oyster advisory — very real for a seaside seafood bar), record the product, lot, affected menu items, action taken (pulled/discarded), and date, producing a documented response trail.

### Mobile-First Manager UX
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | S] Installable PWA (home-screen app, splash, standalone): Add a Web App Manifest (name 'Iggy's Manager', standalone display, theme/background #0a0f0f, maskable 192/512 icons, the existing apple-touch-icon, portrait orientation) plus iOS apple-mobile-web-app meta (already half-present) and an Android maskable icon. Wire a vite-plugin-pwa / Workbox build so the dist ships a manifest + registered service worker. Result: Bradley taps 'Add to Home Screen' once and Iggy's Manager launches full-screen with its own icon and splash, no browser chrome, no URL bar.
- [P0 | wow4 util5 | M] Offline-tolerant shell + read cache (cooler-proof): Service-worker app-shell precache (stale-while-revalidate for the JS/CSS/icons) so the app boots instantly with zero network, plus a runtime cache of the last-known data Bradley actually reads behind the bar: today's parties, today's events, low-stock items, recent messages, the menu. Show a subtle 'Offline — showing last synced 6:12pm' banner instead of a blank error screen.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | L] Offline write queue (optimistic actions that sync later): An outbox: when offline (or on a flaky tap), writes Bradley makes — toggle a special active, mark a to-do done, adjust inventory after a keg blows, log a waste, mark a message read — are committed optimistically to local state and queued in IndexedDB, then flushed to Supabase the moment signal returns (Background Sync where supported, foreground replay otherwise). A small 'pending sync (3)' chip shows queue depth; conflicts surface a toast.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | L] Web push notifications (booking, low-stock, Luna): Web Push (VAPID) wired through a new Supabase Edge Function + a tiny push_subscriptions table. The home-lab Luna bridge — which already writes luna_insights and reads messages/parties on a schedule — triggers pushes: 'New private-event inquiry from Sarah (Sat, 40 guests)', 'Tito's below par — reorder?', 'Luna's 7am briefing is ready', 'Party tonight: 6pm downstairs, 30 guests, deposit unpaid.' Tapping the notification deep-links straight to that party/message/insight. Permission is requested contextually (after first install, with a one-line value prompt), never on first load.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | M] Command palette + global search (Cmd/Ctrl-K and a search tab): A cmdk-style palette that fuzzy-searches across EVERYTHING: navigate to any of the 18 routes, jump to a specific party/contact/event/menu item by name, and run actions ('New event', 'Quick post', 'Mark all messages read', 'Add low-stock items to order', 'Ask Luna…'). On desktop it's Cmd-K; on mobile it's a persistent search affordance (e.g. long-press the center FAB, or a search pill at the top of the dashboard) since phones have no keyboard chord. Recent + frequent actions float to the top.
- [P2 | wow4 util5 | M] Customizable quick-action launcher (long-press FAB radial + home-screen shortcuts): Upgrade the existing center FAB from 'Quick Add Party only' to a launcher: a quick tap still adds a party, but a long-press fans out a radial/sheet of the 5-6 actions Bradley uses most (Quick Post, New Event, Log Inventory, New To-Do, Ask Luna, Scan Order). Make the set reorderable so Bradley pins HIS workflow. Plus manifest 'shortcuts' so a long-press on the installed home-screen icon jumps straight to Quick Post or New Party from outside the app.
- [P2 | wow3 util5 | M] Behind-the-bar speed + one-handed gesture layer: A bundle of native-feeling touches: (1) pull-to-refresh on the list pages instead of relying on Realtime alone; (2) swipe-actions on rows (swipe a message to archive, a to-do to complete, a party to call) reusing the BottomSheet drag mechanics; (3) edge swipe-back navigation; (4) a global haptics helper so taps/confirms buzz app-wide (today vibrate() only fires in the canvas editor); (5) a 'big-tap / one-handed' density toggle that enlarges primary touch targets toward 48-56px for wet-thumb use; (6) instant route transitions via prefetch + skeletons so nothing feels like a page load.
- [P2 | wow2 util3 | S] App-update prompt + version freshness control: Because a service worker caches the app, add a 'New version available — tap to update' toast (the standard Workbox skipWaiting/clientsClaim refresh prompt) so Bradley is never stuck on a stale cached build, plus a tiny build-stamp in Settings so he can confirm what's live during the owner demo.

### Events & Specials Automation Engine
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] One-Action Multi-Channel Publish: Replace the single manual 'share to Instagram' share-sheet with a real Publish hub. From an event or a finished special, one tap fans the asset out to: public site (already wired), Google Calendar (already wired), AND a queued social post (IG/Facebook feed + story). Per-channel copy is auto-generated (full caption + hashtags for IG, short blurb for site, clean title for calendar) and editable before send. A new edge function (publish-social) + a publish_jobs table holds the queue; the Luna bridge or a Meta Graph API token does the actual auto-post, so it goes out even when Bradley's phone is in his pocket.
- [P0 | wow4 util5 | M] Specials Auto-Expire & Auto-Schedule Window: Add starts_at / expires_at (and optional daily time window) to specials. A special can be set to go live Friday 4pm and auto-retire Sunday close — the public site only shows it inside the window, no manual toggle. A nightly Luna/edge sweep flips active off at expiry and (optionally) pulls the social post or posts a 'last call' story. The Specials list groups into Live now / Scheduled / Expired with a countdown chip.
- [P0 | wow3 util5 | L] Recurring-Event Rules Engine (real RRULE + auto-instancing): Upgrade events beyond today's single recurring_day string to a proper rule: weekly/biweekly/monthly, multi-day (e.g. 'Thu + Sat'), date ranges (summer-only series), blackout dates (skip the holiday), and an end date. The engine materializes the next N concrete occurrences so the calendar, availability/conflict logic, and social queue all see real dated instances — not one ghost row. Editing the series vs. a single night is a clear choice (this-and-future / just-this-one), the same model Google Calendar uses.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | M] Happy-Hour Automation (live state, not a static page): Make happy hour a scheduled STATE driven by the existing happy_hour table + time windows. Define the daily window (e.g. Mon–Fri 3–6pm) once; the public HappyHour page and Home banner auto-flip to a live 'Happy Hour ON — ends in 47 min' state during the window and a 'Back tomorrow at 3' state outside it. Optional auto-actions at window start: post an IG story, ping a low-traffic alert. Holiday/blackout overrides supported.
- [P1 | wow5 util4 | L] Themed-Night Playbooks: A playbook bundles everything a recurring night needs into one reusable object: the event rule (Trivia, every Wed), its linked specials (the auto-expiring 'Quiz Night' drink), a pre-built social-post template from the Specials studio, a prep checklist with an owner (set up speakers, print question sheets, brief FOH), and a publish cadence (auto-post the reminder 24h before). Launch a playbook once and every week it self-assembles: events created, specials scheduled, post queued, checklist spawned as todos.
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | M] Weekly Programming Planner: A single Mon–Sun board (mobile-first, swipeable) that shows everything programmed: recurring nights, one-offs, live/scheduled specials, happy-hour windows, and private parties from the Parties module — all on one grid. Drag to reschedule, tap a gap to drop in a playbook, see conflicts (two events same space same night) flagged inline. A 'Publish week' action pushes the whole week's social queue at once. Doubles as the owner's at-a-glance 'what's happening at Iggy's this week.'
- [P1 | wow5 util5 | M] Luna Programming Co-Pilot & Proactive Cadences: Wire the events/specials engine into the home-lab Luna bridge as a domain expert. Luna gets read access to events/specials/happy_hour/parties + a knowledge pack on Iggy's programming, then runs proactive cadences via luna_insights: 'Wednesday is empty next week — want me to drop in the Trivia playbook?', 'Your Sunset special expires tonight, draft a last-call story?', 'It'll be 78F and sunny Saturday — push happy hour harder?'. Luna drafts captions and queues posts by writing publish_jobs rows; Bradley approves with one tap (or sets trusted playbooks to auto-go). Weather + slow-night signals tune the suggestions.
- [P2 | wow3 util3 | M] Two-Way Calendar Subscribe (pull + push): Upgrade from the current one-shot Google Calendar promotion / .ics export to a real subscribable feed: a public webcal/.ics endpoint customers can subscribe to (auto-updates when the program changes), plus genuine two-way sync so an event created in Google Calendar flows into the app (the existing PromoteEventModal already pulls from Google — close the loop so changes propagate both directions and recurring rules round-trip).
- [P2 | wow4 util4 | L] Post-Performance Loop (what actually filled seats): Close the loop by tagging each published event/special with lightweight outcome signals — social reach/saves from the Graph API, and (where available) a rough sales lift window from the POS or a manual 'how'd it go' tap. The planner then surfaces 'Trivia averages 40% above a normal Wednesday' and 'the Espresso Martini special outperforms; the Frosé special underperforms,' and Luna uses it to recommend which playbooks to keep, kill, or reschedule.

### Financials & Invoicing
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | L] Stripe deposit links on event invoices (deposit → balance → paid lifecycle): Wire the already-installed Stripe SDK into a real checkout/edge function so an event invoice generates a hosted payment link for a deposit (e.g. 25% or a flat $200 room hold) and later the balance. Add persisted invoice fields the schema is missing today — invoice_number, deposit_amount, amount_paid, balance_due, payment_status (unpaid/deposit_paid/paid_in_full), paid_at — and update them automatically from Stripe webhooks. The InvoicePanel 'Email invoice' button gains a 'Request deposit' / 'Send balance due' action; Parties list and Invoices page show a live paid/owed badge instead of just a grand total.
- [P0 | wow5 util5 | M] Luna Morning Money Briefing (daily financial insight cadence): A scheduled luna_insights 'briefing' that lands on the Dashboard each morning: yesterday's events revenue + receivables movement, deposits collected, balances now due, vendor bills coming due this week, and an owner-friendly cash snapshot (money in vs. money out). Because there is no POS feed, v1 is built entirely from data Iggy's already owns — party totals, paid/owed state, order-scanner vendor spend. Luna reasons over the live tables and writes the card; tapping it opens the relevant page. Add a weekly 'Sunday close' briefing summarizing the week.
- [P1 | wow3 util4 | S] Tax & service-charge handling on invoices (2026-compliant): Add a configurable sales-tax line and a distinction between 'gratuity (tip, optional)' and 'service charge (mandatory)' on the invoice engine. Oregon has no state sales tax, so default tax to 0% but make it a real, editable line so the invoice is correct and future-proof; critically, flag mandatory service charges separately because under 2026 'No Tax on Tips' rules auto-gratuities/service charges do NOT count as qualified tips. Persist the tax/service-charge breakdown on the invoice record and show it on the printable HTML/PDF.
- [P1 | wow4 util4 | M] Event tip-out / gratuity reconciliation worksheet: When an event closes, take the collected gratuity (or the tip portion of payment) and split it across staff on the shift via configurable tip-out rules (e.g. bartenders 70% / barbacks 20% / event lead 10%), with a hard guardrail that managers/supervisors can never be included (FLSA, no exceptions). Output a per-person payout sheet, store it, and tag mandatory service charges as NON-tip income so payroll/W-2 reporting is clean for the 2026 separate-qualified-tip requirement.
- [P1 | wow4 util5 | M] Vendor-bill ledger built on the Order Scanner: Promote the order scanner from a one-off inventory-matching tool into an accounts-payable ledger. Extend 'orders' (or a new vendor_bills table) with vendor, bill_total, due_date, paid status, and category (liquor/beer/food/supplies). The scanner already extracts supplier-invoice line items — now roll them up into 'owed this week', a per-vendor spend trend, and a 'bills due' list on the dashboard. Let Luna flag 'liquor spend up 22% vs your 4-week average' or 'Sysco bill due Friday'.
- [P2 | wow5 util4 | L] Owner P&L-lite + cash-flow snapshot dashboard: A single owner-facing screen that assembles a lightweight P&L from data the app owns: revenue (events paid + balances due, plus a manual or POS-imported daily bar-sales figure), COGS (vendor-bill spend by category + inventory usage at cost_per_unit), gratuity/tips out, and a net cash position. Benchmark live against 2026 standards — prime cost 55-65%, pour cost 18-24%, beverage cost 15-30% — with color-coded 'on target / over' chips. Include a manual 'daily bar sales' quick-entry (or CSV/POS import) so the top line isn't blank until a POS integration exists.
- [P2 | wow4 util3 | M] Event-pipeline budgeting & forecast (booked vs. target revenue): Set a monthly/quarterly events revenue target and track booked (confirmed party grand totals) + weighted pipeline (inquiries discounted by likelihood) against it, with a simple forecast line. Show 'pacing' — e.g. 'June is 78% to target, 3 open inquiries worth $4.2k would close the gap.' Luna proactively nudges: 'two high-value inquiries have no follow-up and your month is behind target.'

## Gap Backlog (by domain)

### Daily Operations Cockpit
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Shift Sessions — open/close the bar as a first-class object -> A shift_sessions table (opened_by, opened_at, mod_user, spaces_live[], expected_covers, weather_snapshot, status open/closed, closed_at, closed_by) plus a big 'Open the Bar / Close the Bar' control on the Dashboard. Every checklist tick, line-check reading, log entry, drawer count, and the EON report carries a shift_id FK. Close is gated on closing checklist + drawer reconciliation done, then triggers EON generation. This is the spine every other cockpit feature plugs into.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Opening & Closing checklists with photo proof + completion audit -> checklist_templates + checklist_template_items (item_type: tick | number | photo_required) editable in-app like MenuManager, and checklist_runs + checklist_run_items (shift_id FK, done_by, done_at, value, photo_url) for the live instance. Reusable Open/Close + upstairs/downstairs lists, a Dashboard progress ring ('Closing 14/22'), and a hard rule that a run can't be marked complete with required items skipped. Photo items hit Supabase Storage exactly like the existing image upload.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Line Check with auto-fail temps + corrective-task escalation -> line_check_templates (items with safe min/max ranges: keg level, fridge/cooler temps, CO2, ice, garnish freshness) + line_check_runs (shift_id FK, per-item reading). An out-of-range number auto-fails, turns red, and auto-creates a corrective task assigned to the MOD with a deadline; if still open at close it escalates onto the owner's Dashboard and into the EON report. Pass/fail history charts per item so a slowly-warming cooler is visible before it spoils a weekend's stock.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Shift Log & Manager-on-Duty journal (with 86 + pass-down hooks) -> A shift_log table (shift_id FK, tag enum: 86 | incident | comp_void | refused_service | VIP | maintenance | staff | weather, body text, photo_url) that is filterable across dates ('how many ice-machine fails this month' = one query). 86-ing a menu item from here flips an availability flag on the matching MenuManager row. Unresolved threads carry to the next MOD as pass-down notes. This is the richest free-text Luna can mine for patterns.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Cash / Till reconciliation with over-short tracking (manual-first) -> cash_counts (shift_id FK, drawer, starting_bank, denomination breakdown, expected_total manual-entered now, computed variance/over-short, non-cash tender, deposit, counted_by + mod signatures). Variances over a threshold force a required note before the shift can close; over/short trends chart per drawer and per staffer. Built manual-'expected'-first with a clean seam to import 'expected' from Toast/Square later — don't block the highest-trust feature on a POS integration that doesn't exist.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) End-of-Night report — one tap, auto-composed, owner-delivered -> On close, the cockpit assembles one eon_reports row per shift: sales/covers, over/short, checklist completion %, line-check fails + open correctives, 86'd items, incidents, weather, notable log entries, tomorrow's heads-up (parties/events/deliveries). Luna drafts the narrative on top of the structured numbers (written back like a luna_insight); Bradley reviews/edits/signs and it emails the owner via the existing Gmail edge function, archived as a browsable per-shift record — before he walks out. THE board moment.
- (partial, tier1, impact5, S) Daily Run-Sheet & Pre-Shift Huddle card -> An auto-built single screen composing today's events + confirmed parties + active specials/happy-hour + low-stock/pending deliveries + weather + a covers/staffing projection, with a generated 3-5 bullet Pre-Shift Huddle summary the MOD reads to staff (Luna can expand/voice it). Mostly composition over data the app already holds — the natural home for the 07:00 briefing. Quick win relative to its visible impact; mainly needs a weather fetch added.
- (partial, tier2, impact4, M) Per-shift task assignment & roles (beyond the owner<->manager Todos board) -> Lightweight per-shift assignment: attach checklist sections, line-check duties, and side-work to a named bartender/server/MOD for tonight (names pulled from Team/manage-users), with a personal 'my tasks' view and tick-off. Recurring side-work (Mon deep-clean taps, Fri restock) auto-populates by day. Gives the EON report a 'who did what' accountability layer. Distinct from, and built on top of, the checklist + shift-session spine.
- (missing, tier3, impact5, L) Offline-first cockpit (installable PWA) for behind-the-bar dead zones -> Ship the operations cockpit as an installable PWA: a service worker + manifest + icons, an offline app shell, and an IndexedDB write-queue for checklist ticks, temps, log entries, photos, and the drawer count that syncs on reconnect, with a visible 'pending sync' indicator. Invisible to the owner but it's what makes the cockpit trustworthy at 1am rather than a fair-weather toy. Larger because it touches build config, sync/conflict logic, and every write path.
- (partial, tier3, impact5, L) Luna as Operations Co-Pilot — photo review, EON drafting, exception/pattern alerts -> Once the spine lands: (a) grant the luna_bridge role READ on shift_sessions/checklists/line_checks/shift_log/cash_counts/eon_reports + a durable Iggy's OPS KNOWLEDGE PACK in her system prompt (par levels, normal over/short range, which coolers run hot, the seaside-week rhythm) so judgments are calibrated; (b) AI photo review of closing photos ('patio chairs not stacked' as a note); (c) the EON-narrative draft job written into eon_reports/luna_insights; (d) proactive cadences beyond 07:00 — a pre-shift 'tonight' card, a real-time over/short alert past threshold, and pattern alerts ('3rd cooler fail this week — call refrigeration before Saturday'). She reviews exceptions; Bradley acts. The single most defensible feature in the app.
- (missing, tier2, impact3, S) Incident & Compliance log (alcohol service / safety) -> A structured 'incident' sub-type of the shift log: refused service / overserve cut-off, ID checks/refusals, slips/injuries, breakages, comps/voids over a threshold, equipment failures — each timestamped, photo-attachable, with optional witness/staff field and a 'reported to owner' flag, routed into the EON report and a standalone incident archive. Low marginal effort once the shift log exists; signals operational maturity in an owner meeting.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Operations history & owner scorecard (analytics) -> A read-mostly analytics view + shareable 'owner scorecard': checklist completion % by week, over/short trend and monthly total variance, line-check fail frequency by station, most-86'd items, incident counts, on-time open/close rates — date-range filtered, sparkline-per-metric. No new data capture, just composition; the capstone that proves the system was worth it. Gated on accumulated data, hence later.

### Inventory Depth & COGS
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Vendor catalogs (suppliers as first-class entities) -> New vendors table (contact, rep email/phone, order method, minimums, delivery days, account #) + vendor_catalog (pack size, case/each, vendor SKU, last price). inventory_items link to one or more catalog lines. Backfill by de-duping the distinct supplier strings already in inventory_items and the supplier names captured in orders.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, L) Par-based auto-reorder with draft purchase orders -> 'Build Order' flow: bundle all below-par items into draft POs grouped by vendor, suggested qty = (par - on_hand) rounded to pack/case size. New purchase_orders + purchase_order_items tables (status draft/sent/received). One tap emails the PO to the rep via a new send-purchase-order edge fn, or copies to clipboard for text-only vendors.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, L) Pour & recipe costing tied to the live menu -> recipes table mapping each menu item (cocktails/on_tap/food) to inventory ingredients with exact yields (1.5oz vodka, 0.75oz lime) + a bottle-yield config (750ml=16.9oz, keg=124 pints). Auto-compute cost-per-drink and pour cost % = ingredient cost / parsed menu price, surfaced inline in MenuManager. Flag cocktails drifting above a target band (>24%) when a linked ingredient's cost rises.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) COGS% & theoretical-vs-actual variance report -> Period report (week/month) computing actual usage from inventory_logs valued at cost_per_unit vs. theoretical usage from recipe map x units sold. Output COGS$/COGS% by category (Liquor/Beer/Wine/Food), variance $ and %, and a ranked 'biggest leaks' list. Period boundaries anchor to dated stocktake snapshots. Show target bands inline (18-24% pour cost, red flag above ~1.5% acceptable variance).
- (partial, tier2, impact5, L) Full stocktake sessions (count-by-area, mobile, fast) -> stocktake_sessions + stocktake_counts model so a count is one dated event. Mobile flow: pick area (Back Bar/Well/Walk-in/Upstairs store), enter counts incl. tenths-of-a-bottle (3.4), optional Bluetooth scale. On close: write a dated valuation snapshot ($ on hand by category), auto-generate count_adjustment logs for deltas (reusing adjustQuantity), and stamp the period boundary the COGS report consumes.
- (partial, tier2, impact4, M) Supplier price-change tracking (off the invoice scanner) -> Capture unit_price per scanned line; on every confirmed scan diff each matched line's unit price vs. the last recorded price for that vendor+product and write a price_history row. Show a sparkline + 'last paid' on the item; raise a luna_insight / alert when a price jumps beyond a threshold (e.g. +5%). Roll affected drinks' pour cost forward so margin flags stay honest.
- (partial, tier1, impact4, M) Waste & spillage log with reasons + cost roll-up -> Quick-capture waste flow: tap item, qty, reason code, optional photo to Supabase Storage. A waste dashboard totals $ wasted by reason and category per period and feeds the variance report's explanation column so 'unexplained variance' shrinks to real shrinkage. Builds directly on inventory_logs.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, L) Seasonal / day-of-week smart par levels -> Track usage velocity from inventory_logs; surface a 'suggested par' the manager accepts or overrides, flexing by season and weekday (summer Friday well-tequila par auto-rises vs. a quiet Feb Tuesday). Tie par suggestions to the events calendar so a booked DJ Night / Karaoke / upstairs party lifts the relevant pars for that week.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) Luna 'COGS Watchdog' proactive insight cadence -> Give the bridge read access to the new tables + a durable Iggy's COGS knowledge pack. Luna writes scheduled luna_insights: a Monday COGS recap that remembers last week and names the specific leaking bottle; an invoice-triggered price-spike alert ('Coastal raised well tequila 9% — 3 cocktails now over 24% pour cost'); pre-busy-week reorder nudges off the events calendar; and 'you haven't counted the back bar in 9 days' reminders. She can draft the PO email or a menu price bump as pending rows for one-tap approval.

### Staff Scheduling & Labor
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Staff roster (employees table + CRUD) -> New `staff` table (name, phone, email, role/position, employment_type, hourly_wage, tip_role_points, cert list, active, hire_date, optional auth user_id link). A Staff page to CRUD them. This is the keystone every other labor feature depends on — without staff rows, nothing else can be built. Keep separate from the auth-manager concept (most staff never log in).
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Staff availability + time-off requests -> `staff_availability` (recurring weekly day/time can-work windows per staff) + `time_off_requests` (date range, reason, status pending/approved/denied). Manager approves/denies; approved rows hard-block that staffer from being scheduled. Feeds the scheduler's eligible-staff filter.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Shift scheduling board (assign shifts to staff/dates) -> `shifts` table (date, start/end, role, staff_id nullable, status, published flag) + a week-grid Schedule page where the manager drops shifts onto day columns and assigns staff. Reuse the minutes-from-midnight/after-midnight time model already proven in events (src/lib/timeWindows.ts) so late bar shifts work. Mobile-first since Bradley runs this on his phone behind the bar. Publish action snapshots the week as the source of truth.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Open shifts + one-tap swaps/claims -> Unassigned shifts published as 'open'; staff (or manager on their behalf) claim them; swap requests between two staff with manager approval. Status flow on the shifts table + a simple claims/swaps table. Kills the cover-shift texting that the research flags as the P0 pain. Notification delivery (SMS) is a separate dependency item.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, L) Staff notifications portal (SMS/push delivery) -> A delivery channel so published schedules, open-shift alerts, and swap approvals actually reach staff phones. Options: (a) Twilio SMS edge function, or (b) a lightweight magic-link staff view (no password) + web push. Research names SMS as the foundation that 'kills cover-shift texting'. Pick SMS for reach; it's the gating dependency for swaps/claims to be useful. New edge function + provider account + cost.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Live labor-percent gauge (labor cost vs forecast sales) -> Gauge on Dashboard: sum(scheduled shift hours x wage) / forecast sales, shown vs a 25-30% target band, color-coded. Requires (1) wages on staff, (2) scheduled hours from shifts, (3) a forecast/expected-sales number. Demo-ready owner artifact. The single most impressive item to show the boss; build it the moment shifts+wages+forecast exist.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, L) Demand forecast (day-part sales prediction) -> A forecast that pre-fills expected sales per day-part from (a) historical patterns by weekday/event-type, (b) the booked parties on that date (huge edge over 7shifts — Iggy's already knows its event book), (c) optional weather. Output is one number per shift block that pre-fills staffing and drives the labor gauge. Start rules-based (event + party + weekday averages); Luna can later refine it. Research explicitly says 'knows the event book, beats 7shifts' — this is the differentiator.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Tip pooling + same-day payout calc (SB 648) -> `tip_pools` (date/shift, total collected) + distribution computed by hours-worked and role points (from staff.tip_role_points), with a same-day payout view per California SB 648. Manager enters the pool total, app splits it and records who got what. Research flags this P0/boardWow=5 — 'ends tip disputes'. Depends on shifts existing to know hours-worked per staffer.
- (missing, tier2, impact3, M) Certification tracking + hard-gate alarms -> Cert fields on staff (type, number, issued, expires) + expiry alarms. A hard gate in the scheduler: a staffer with a lapsed/missing required cert (e.g. RBS for a bartender shift) cannot be assigned/published to that shift. Research calls certs 'a hard gate'. Quick to add once staff rows exist — small table + a validation check in publish.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) SPLH / sales-per-labor-hour analytics -> Sales-per-labor-hour metric per shift/day-part to show the owner which blocks are over/under-staffed and 'what to cut'. Derived once forecast/actual sales and scheduled hours exist; surfaces as a small Dashboard panel and a Luna insight input. Lower priority than the headline gauge but cheap once that infra lands.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, L) Time clock (clock in/out, actual hours) -> `time_punches` (staff_id, shift_id, clock_in, clock_out) via a simple staff-facing or manager-driven punch. Upgrades tip pooling from scheduled-hours to actual-hours and makes the labor gauge reflect reality, not plan. Can launch v1 on scheduled hours and add this later — hence ambitious tier.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Overtime / labor-law guardrails -> On the schedule board, tally each staffer's weekly scheduled hours and flag/block assignments that push them into OT (>8/day or >40/week CA) or violate minor-hours rules. Research lists 'cert/OT staff blocked' as part of the P0 gauge screen. Reuses the shifts data; mostly a computed warning layer at publish time.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) Luna labor co-pilot (draft schedules, debriefs, cert alerts as pending rows) -> Once staff/shifts/availability/tips tables exist, give the bridge read access to them + labor cadences: Luna drafts next week's schedule as pending shift rows the manager approves, posts a post-shift labor debrief (labor% vs target, SPLH), and fires cert-expiry/OT/understaffed-vs-forecast alerts as luna_insights. No new in-app LLM — extend the existing knowledge pack + insight cadence. High leverage, low marginal effort because the bridge is already built; gated on the data tables landing first.

### Private Events & BEOs
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) One-page BEO document generated from the party record -> A 'Generate BEO' action on PartyProfile renders a structured one-page Banquet Event Order (header, setup block, menu/service from food_service_type + party_packages, beverage plan, special_requests, staffing footer) to print/PDF, reusing the buildInvoiceHtml pattern. A beo_documents row (party_id, version, status draft/final, pdf_url in Storage) captures each revision; a BEO-version badge plus a T-30/T-14/T-7/T-2 sign-off checklist (event/kitchen/FOH acknowledged) sit on the profile.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Shareable client proposal + tokenized portal link (no login) -> A 'Send proposal' action on PartyProfile generates a tokenized public read-only page (/p/:token backed by a proposals row + RLS on the token) showing the branded event summary and computeInvoice() itemized pricing, with an 'Accept proposal' button. Status tracking sent/viewed/accepted via a viewed_at ping edge function, surfaced as a badge on the party. Emailed via the existing send-party-email function.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Deposit collection + payment schedule (wire Stripe for parties) -> A create-deposit-checkout edge function opens a Stripe Checkout Session for a configurable deposit (flat or % of grand total); a stripe-webhook function records to a new party_payments table (amount, type deposit/balance, status, stripe_id). PartyProfile gains a Payments card (deposit due/paid/balance due with a status pill) and a payment schedule (deposit on booking, balance T-7) with automated reminder emails. Balance-due/overdue states feed the dashboard.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, L) E-signature contract on the proposal -> A contracts table (party_id, template, rendered_html, signer_name, signed_at, typed/drawn signature, signed_ip, status sent/viewed/signed) plus a reusable single-signer venue-contract template filled via fillTemplate. The client signs on the portal page; a sign-contract edge function captures signature + timestamp + IP and writes the signed PDF to Storage. PartyProfile shows signature status + 'Download signed contract'; signing can auto-advance the party to confirmed. Pairs with deposit in one 'Accept & sign & pay' flow.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Run-of-show / day-of timeline builder -> A party_timeline table (time/offset, who FOH/kitchen/bar/DJ, action, notes) rendered as an ordered list on PartyProfile, seeded ~80% from the chosen preset (extend partyPresets.ts with timeline templates). Renders into the BEO and into a large-tap phone 'day-of view' Bradley runs from behind the bar with the next cue highlighted. Highest daily-use feature for the manager.
- (partial, tier1, impact4, M) Online-bookable packages with live price estimator on the public site -> Add a live estimate calculator to BookEvent (pick package + guest count + hours -> instant total using the same computeInvoice logic, ported to the public app) and add public_description + featured columns to packages so the public cards are branded/curated separately from internal notes. Pre-qualifies leads with budget + headcount before the request lands.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, XL) Visual 2D floor plan / room layout per event -> A lightweight 2D layout editor for upstairs/downstairs: pre-drawn room shells (Storage SVG background) with drag-and-drop table/bar/DJ/dancefloor tokens snapped to a grid, saved per party as layout JSON + flattened PNG (party_layouts row), embedded in the BEO and shareable on the proposal. Ship with reusable room TEMPLATES ('Upstairs cocktail', 'Upstairs seated 40') so most events pick-and-tweak. Full 3D explicitly out of scope.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) BEO change-log + version diff with re-acknowledge (change orders) -> Track material changes to a confirmed party (guest_count, menu, timeline, space, totals) as beo_change rows; once the BEO is final, a threshold-crossing change becomes a 'change order' that resets kitchen/FOH/event sign-offs to pending and can fire an updated-BEO email. PartyProfile shows a compact change history ('guest count 30 -> 45, +$340, by Bradley, 2 days out'). Makes the BEO trustworthy as the single source of truth.
- (missing, tier1, impact3, S) Tasting & site-visit scheduling tied to the party -> A small scheduling block on PartyProfile for pre-event touchpoints (tasting, site visit, final-details call): pick a slot, write a calendar event via the existing google-calendar function, send a confirmation via send-party-email; these appear on the unified Calendar alongside the event and feed the follow-up cadence. Low-effort glue that makes the bigger bookings feel managed end to end.
- (partial, tier3, impact5, L) Bridge-Luna events playbook: knowledge pack + proactive cadences + row-level drafting -> Give the bridge a durable events knowledge pack + read access to parties/contacts/party_packages/packages and the new proposals/contracts/party_payments/beo/timeline/layout tables. Add five proactive insight cadences into luna_insights: lead-SLA alert, deposit/balance watch, BEO-readiness brief, proposal-viewed-but-cold nudge, daily morning events briefing. Let Luna draft first-pass proposal + BEO + run-of-show rows seeded from the matching preset for Bradley's one-tap approval — never a chatbot, always drafting/nudging via rows.

### Social Media & Content Marketing
- (missing, tier2, impact5, XL) True multi-platform auto-publish (replace the manual share sheet) -> A social_posts queue table (status draft/scheduled/publishing/posted/failed, target_platforms[], asset_url, per_platform_caption jsonb, scheduled_at, external_post_ids jsonb, error). A new social-publish edge function (Deno) holding OAuth tokens that does real API posting: Instagram Graph (POST /media container -> /media_publish for feed/Reels/Stories/carousel), Facebook Page API, Google Business Profile posts fully auto; TikTok via pull-from-inbox draft for v1 (direct-post needs an audited app). One 'Publish'/'Schedule' button in SpecialEditor with platform toggle chips; the existing export feeds straight in; status + permalink written back to the row and shown in-app.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Content Calendar that auto-drafts from Iggy's own event/special/happy_hour data -> A week/month social calendar view over social_posts. A 'Fill my week' button reads events, specials, happy_hour, parties and auto-drafts a ready-to-approve queue (Thu GBP+IG 'Trivia tonight 8pm' from the recurring event; Fri 'Happy Hour 4-7' from happy_hour; Sat 'DJ Night' from events). Drag to reschedule, color-coded by platform, gaps highlighted ('no post Tuesday'). Each draft is one tap into the auto-publish pipeline. This is the unfair advantage no off-the-shelf tool can match.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Luna AI caption + hashtag + best-time engine (bridge-native) -> A 'Draft with Luna' button on each special/event draft writes a luna_messages (or dedicated social-draft) row with full context; home-lab Luna (Iggy's knowledge pack: brand voice, menu, recurring schedule, hashtag sets, cadence) replies with a per-platform caption set (punchy IG/TikTok hook, fuller FB, GBP with a hard 'Click to book' CTA), a curated local+nightlife+seasonal-seaside hashtag block, and a recommended post time from past engagement. Manager edits inline or accepts. Pure bridge play, no in-app LLM.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) UGC inbox + one-tap repost-permission flow + Social Bank -> A 'Tagged' tab pulling @mentions and tagged photos/Reels via IG/FB Graph mentions endpoints. Each item shows the customer post; 'Request to repost' fires a templated permission DM/comment ('Love this! Can we feature it? Reply YES'); on YES the asset drops into a 'Social Bank' (MediaLibrary folder, ugc=true, rights_granted_at timestamp). One tap from there into a Story/feed post. A 'Tag @iggys to get featured' QR table-tent generator seeds the funnel. Strategy: MIX banked UGC with original posts (Meta's 2026 algo demotes pure reposters).
- (partial, tier2, impact3, M) Live link-in-bio page powered by the real site DB -> A public /l/iggys micro-page rendering LIVE from Supabase: tonight's event, this week's happy hour, menu link, 'Book your party' (deep-link into BookEvent + online package booking), shop, reservations, directions. Auto-reorders by relevance (event tonight floats to top). One manager-app toggle sets the 'featured' button. UTM-tagged so clicks appear in analytics. Low-effort because it is mostly a new view over existing pages/data; routes straight into the owner's package-booking and Stripe-deposit asks.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) Story template pack tied to today's event (1080x1920) -> Extend the existing template engine with Stories templates sized 1080x1920: 'Tonight' countdown, 'Happy Hour now', 'We're open', 'Last call', poll/question stickers, 'Tag us' frame, weather-aware 'Perfect patio night'. Templates auto-fill from events/happy_hour (tonight's DJ name, time, special price) so a full Story is one tap. Schedulable through the auto-publish queue (IG/FB Stories via Graph API). High-leverage reuse of existing tech, not a rebuild.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, L) Post performance dashboard + Luna weekly insight cadence -> A social_post_metrics table fed from platform Insights APIs (IG/FB Insights, GBP Insights: views/clicks/calls/direction-requests). A per-post analytics card and a Dashboard roll-up tile ('This week: 6 posts, 4.2k reach, Trivia Reel was top'). Home-lab Luna reads social_post_metrics on a schedule and pushes a luna_insights row every Monday: what worked, what to repeat, which night is underposted, suggested themes — proactively. Closes the loop: measurement feeds next week's auto-drafted calendar (recycle-your-winners).
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) 'Turn tonight into a Reel' short-form vertical video flow -> A lightweight 'turn tonight into a Reel' capture-and-post flow: pick a phone clip, auto-trim to the 60-90s favored band, optional caption/sticker overlay pulled from tonight's event, Luna-drafted caption + hashtags, publish to IG Reels/TikTok/Shorts via the auto-publish queue. Deliberately makes raw, friend-showing-you-something content trivial to post rather than gating everything behind the design studio.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Approval / guard rails / scheduled-queue safety -> A publish-guard layer: drafts markable 'needs owner approval' (surface on the Todos board), token-expiry warnings ('Reconnect Instagram'), a 'pause all scheduled' kill switch, failed-post retry with a clear error ('TikTok rejected: video too short'), and a media-rights gate so a UGC asset cannot auto-publish without rights_granted set. Low glamour, high trust — the safety story is what makes 'automatic' feel responsible to the owner.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, S) One-tap 'Post to Social' entry point on Events (not just Specials) -> A 'Post to Social' / 'Draft a post' action on each event (and on happy_hour rows) that pre-loads the canvas/template with the event's title, time, space, and category, then routes into the Luna caption + auto-publish flow. Makes the bar's existing event DB a one-tap content source, eliminating the blank-page rebuild. This is the cheapest bridge between the existing data and the new pipeline.
- (missing, tier1, impact3, S) Per-platform caption variant editor (one composer, many formats) -> A single composer that holds one post and shows editable per-platform variants (TikTok hook vs Facebook context vs GBP hard-CTA vs IG), driven by the per_platform_caption jsonb on social_posts and pre-filled by the Luna engine. Uniform cross-posting is a 2026 anti-pattern; this makes write-once / reformat-per-platform the default. Small once social_posts + Luna output schema exist — it is mostly the editing UI over that data.

### Website CMS, SEO & Local SEO
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Schema auto-sync: Menu/MenuItem, Event & FAQ JSON-LD generated from Supabase -> An edge function (on-write trigger + build hook) reads the existing menu/events tables and emits valid Restaurant+Menu+MenuItem (with prices), Event JSON-LD for every active upcoming event (name, startDate from date+start_min, location, offers), and a FAQPage block (hours, parking, dog policy, private-event capacity). Injected into prerendered per-route HTML, not client-side. Manager 'Schema' tab shows each page's live JSON-LD, a one-click Rich Results validity check, and last-pushed timestamp. Self-maintaining: a price or DJ-night edit updates schema automatically.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Per-route SEO via prerender/SSG (kill the one-head-for-every-page SPA) -> Add vite-react-ssg (or prerender step) so each public route ships server-rendered HTML with unique title, meta description, canonical, OG image, and route-specific JSON-LD. Pair with a manager 'Page SEO' editor: per-page title/meta/OG with live Google+social preview, character-count guidance, and a Luna 'suggest' button. This is the foundation that makes schema, FAQ and AEO actually visible to crawlers and AI bots.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Core Web Vitals rescue: responsive WebP/AVIF pipeline + auto-compress on upload -> Convert heroes/features to responsive AVIF/WebP with width-based srcset, explicit width/height (no CLS), lazy-load below fold, preload the LCP hero. Add Supabase Storage image transform so any MediaLibrary upload is auto-resized/compressed (can't regress). Manager 'Site Health' card shows live LCP/INP/CLS per page from PageSpeed/CrUX with red/yellow/green and plain-English fixes. Highest ROI-per-hour technical win; seaside walk-up traffic is overwhelmingly phones on cellular and March-2026 core update tightened thresholds.
- (partial, tier1, impact3, S) Host signal fix (www vs apex) + dynamic sitemap auto-generation -> Unify to one host (apex) across robots/canonical/OG. Replace the static sitemap with an edge function that generates sitemap.xml from live routes + DB content with accurate lastmod, and pings Google on publish. The host fix is a near-zero-effort quick win; the dynamic sitemap keeps time-sensitive events indexable before they go stale.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Google Business Profile cockpit inside the manager app -> GBP API panel in the manager app: read/reply to reviews (Luna-drafted), read Insights (search views, direction requests, calls, photo views, website clicks), post GBP Posts straight from existing Specials/Events, update hours+holiday hours once and sync, and a NAP-consistency checker flagging GBP vs site-schema drift.
