GBP is the #1 local ranking factor and 2026 weights engagement signals; folding it into the app Bradley already runs behind the bar makes daily upkeep a 30-second habit and gives the owner a proof-of-traffic dashboard.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Luna AI-Search & Citation Monitor (AEO/GEO for 'Seaside, Oregon') -> Luna insight cadence that periodically asks AI answer engines (AI Overviews/ChatGPT Search/Perplexity) real customer questions ('best seaside bar Seaside OR', 'live music Seaside tonight', 'dog-friendly bar Oregon coast', 'happy hour near the Promenade'), reports whether Iggy's is named/cited and what's wrong or stale, and drafts the fix (FAQ entry, schema field, GBP post, About line) as a one-tap luna_insights suggestion row. Plus a quarterly NAP/entity-consistency audit across site, GBP and directories. Cutting-edge, plays to the Luna bridge, and lands hard in an owner pitch — almost no competitor bar does this.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Search Console health feed + Indexing API auto-ping for events -> Pull GSC into the dashboard: indexed-vs-submitted count, top queries/clicks/impressions, coverage errors, per-page index status with one-tap 'Request indexing'. Auto-ping Google's Indexing API on event publish so events index before they're stale. Top-query data tells Bradley exactly which dishes/events people search, feeding the schema and content work. Turns 'is Google even seeing us?' into a daily glance.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Conversion & local-intent landing pages (private-events / book CRO) -> Prerendered local-intent landing pages (private events, oceanfront venue, live music tonight) each with Event/FAQ schema, a sticky Book/Get-Directions/Call bar, and a clear above-the-fold CTA. Add CRO instrumentation the manager can read (booking-form starts vs completes, CTA CTR), an A/B toggle on the BookEvent hero, and prominent mobile click-to-call + Maps deep link. Turns SEO from a vanity metric into countable bookings.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, M) Accessibility (a11y) pass for SEO + ADA, with alt-text capture in MediaLibrary -> Manager-app a11y checklist driven by a periodic axe-core scan (plain-language score + top issues): alt text on every image (auto-prompt on MediaLibrary upload, Luna drafts descriptive alt from filename/context), contrast fixes on the dark theme, focus states + keyboard nav, proper heading hierarchy/landmarks, accessible form labels, and a skip-to-content link. Overlaps SEO/AEO heavily (semantic HTML + alt text are read by crawlers and image-capable AI models) and reduces real ADA risk.

### Reservations, Waitlist & Tables
- (missing, tier1, impact5, L) Core data model: tables/sections, reservations, waitlist, covers, guest-profile (schema + migrations) -> New Supabase tables: `sections` (bar rail / hi-tops / patio / upstairs / downstairs, open/closed flag, capacity), `floor_tables` (label, section_id, seats, x/y coords, shape), `reservations` (date, slot start/end_min, party_size, status, section/table refs, deposit state, contact ref), `waitlist_entries` (name, phone, party_size, quoted_wait, status, joined_at, notified_at), and `guest_profiles` (or extend contacts) keyed by phone. RLS + realtime enabled. This is the foundation every other item depends on.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, L) Virtual text-to-join waitlist (host stand on phone) -> New manager route /waitlist: live color-coded list (sorted by wait), add-in-two-taps, one-tap 'Notify — table ready' firing an SMS via a new send-sms edge function (Twilio). Public QR/web page lets walk-ins self-add (name, party size, phone) and see 'you're #4, ~25 min'. Two-way SMS inbound webhook so 'HERE'/'OMW'/'CANCEL' auto-moves them. Realtime list shared across staff devices.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) SMS rail: Twilio send + two-way inbound webhook edge functions -> New `send-sms` Deno edge function (Twilio REST) plus an inbound `sms-webhook` function that maps replies (HERE/OMW/CANCEL/confirm) back to waitlist_entries / reservations by phone. Shared by waitlist notify, reservation confirm/remind, and Luna-drafted sends. A `sms_log` table for delivery state. This is the load-bearing dependency for waitlist + reservation reminders.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, L) Deposits & credit-card holds on large groups / packages (real Stripe) -> New `create-checkout`/`create-hold` edge function issuing Stripe Checkout Session (deposit) or SetupIntent (card-on-file hold). Owner-set rules (flat $200, or $25/head for 8+). Deposit/hold state stored on reservation + party records; webhook function (`stripe-webhook`) to confirm/capture/release. Directly answers two owner open-asks (online package booking + real Stripe). Same rail also un-stubs merch checkout.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, XL) Timed online table reservations (public widget + per-slot capacity) -> New public reservation widget (separate from BookEvent): pick date / party size / time SLOT against real per-section, per-slot covers capacity so the room can't be oversold. Confirmation + reminder via existing Gmail edge fn and new SMS rail. Flows into the floor plan and a manager day/agenda view. Reuses time-slot UI + space model; adds the covers + capacity-rules dimension.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, L) Walk-in & regular guest CRM (notes, tags, allergies, VIP, 86'd, visit count) -> Extend contacts into a true guest_profile covering walk-ins/diners: per-visit notes, dietary/allergy flags, free + AUTO tags (VIP, Regular, Birthday, Big Tipper, 86'd, repeat-no-show), last_visit + visit_count, favorite seat/drink. Every reservation + waitlist entry match-or-creates by phone so personalization needs zero host data entry. Feeds Luna's outreach drafting.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) No-show & late-cancel status + policy + per-guest history -> Add 'no_show' + 'late_cancel' statuses to reservations and parties. One-tap mark-no-show that (per the deposit feature) captures the deposit / charges the held card and stamps the guest profile, incrementing a per-guest no-show counter. Cancellation-policy line shown at booking + in reminders; daily 'who didn't show' rollup; repeat-no-show flag surfaced at booking time.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Unified host 'tonight' board (reservations + waitlist + events + covers on one screen) -> A single mobile cover-flow 'tonight' view: upcoming reservations, live waitlist, any event/party in the space, current covers seated + turn timers, with a top-line 'on the books tonight: 86 covers / 4 large groups / DJ upstairs at 9'. The screen Bradley keeps open all shift; ties the new pieces into one product. Strong board demo.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, XL) Drag-and-drop floor plan with sections, turn timers & door pacing -> Visual map of the real room (bar rail, hi-tops, patio/deck, upstairs, downstairs) with tappable tables showing status (open/seated/check-dropped/needs-bussing) and a live turn timer each. Drag a waitlist/reservation chip onto a table to seat. Pacing guardrail warns when too many parties land in one 15-min window so the single bar + small kitchen don't get buried. Sections open/close as staffing changes. The 'wow' board demo.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) Luna front-of-house copilot: wait quotes, big-group triage, no-show nudges, drafted confirms -> Teach the bridge Iggy's room + turn times + deposit/no-show policy (knowledge pack); give live READ on the new reservation/waitlist/table/covers/guest tables; add proactive luna_insights cadences (morning 'tonight on the books' covers+large-groups+unpaid-deposits+weather-vs-patio; real-time 'list is 40 min deep, open the patio / quote 35'; 'this group has 2 prior no-shows, take a card'); and act-by-writing-rows: Luna drafts personalized confirm/reminder SMS+email from guest-profile notes into the send queues for one-tap approval. The moat an off-the-shelf Resy can't match.

### Payments & Online Commerce
- (missing, tier1, impact5, L) Real Stripe Checkout (hosted Checkout Sessions) + live Shop storefront -> Live Shop page renders the existing MerchCard grid + CartDrawer. CartDrawer POSTs the cart to a new Supabase edge fn 'create-checkout-session' that builds a Stripe Checkout Session (mode:'payment', automatic_payment_methods:true → free Apple Pay/Google Pay/Link/Cash App) and 302-redirects to Stripe's hosted page. A 'stripe-webhook' edge fn verifies signature on checkout.session.completed and writes a real order row. Success/cancel return URLs land back on the site.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, M) DB-backed merch products with manager CRUD -> A merch_products table (name, price, description, image_url, sizes[], active, tax_code, inventory_count?) seeded from the 3 current items. A Manager 'Merch' CRUD screen reusing the proven MenuManager pattern (create/edit/price/toggle-active/upload-image via MediaLibrary/Storage). Public Shop reads live from the table so the owner sees new drops appear without a deploy.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Unified customer Orders ledger + fulfillment ops screen -> New customer_orders + order_items tables (named to avoid the inventory `orders` collision) written by the webhook on checkout.session.completed. A Manager 'Orders' route: each paid order is a card with line items, fulfillment_type (pickup|shipping), status (paid→preparing→ready→fulfilled/shipped), customer contact, tracking-number field. Pickup orders get a 'Mark Ready' button firing a ready-for-pickup email/SMS; shipping orders capture the Stripe address and let Bradley paste a tracking number that auto-emails the buyer. Realtime subscription buzzes the dashboard on a new order — reuses Parties/Inventory card+drawer+realtime patterns.
- (partial, tier2, impact3, M) Cart hardening: tax line, shipping, persistence, discount codes -> Cart persists to localStorage so it survives refresh/return. Tax and shipping are computed by Stripe at the Checkout Session (so the in-app cart shows 'taxes & shipping calculated at checkout') rather than guessed client-side. Optional promo/discount code field maps to Stripe coupons/promotion_codes. CartDrawer footer shows subtotal + a clear 'tax/shipping at next step' note instead of a bare total.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Stripe Tax on every checkout + sales-tax line on party invoices -> automatic_tax:{enabled:true} on every Checkout Session and on party/deposit payments so tax is computed at the delivery address (Oregon pickup/storefront = $0 correctly; merch SHIPPED to WA/CA/NY collects the right destination rate). Product tax codes set (txcd_30070000 apparel taxable, txcd_10503000 gift cards non-taxable at sale, food per local rules). Add an explicit sales-tax line to computeInvoice/buildInvoiceHtml/buildInvoiceText so event invoices are legally clean. A small Tax Settings panel (origin address, registered states, codes). Note: Stripe applies your Tax settings by default from Apr 29 2026, so this is table stakes once checkout is live.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Event deposits & no-show protection (manual-capture auth holds) + paid/balance ledger -> Add deposit_amount, deposit_status, payment_intent_id, paid_total, balance_due to parties. On BookEvent and inside a Party record, request a deposit as either a true prepayment (charge now, e.g. 50% down) or a manual-capture PaymentIntent (capture_method:'manual') that authorizes/holds the card and only captures on a no-show/late-cancel in the policy window. InvoicePanel gains 'Deposit paid', 'Balance due', and a 'Send payment link' button (Stripe Payment Link) so a 40-person party settles the balance online instead of fumbling a reader. Deposit confirmation email. Auth holds auto-release ~7 days — surface that.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, L) Digital gift cards (buy online, redeem in-house) -> gift_cards + gift_card_transactions tables and a public 'Gift Cards' page: buyer picks $25/$50/$100/custom, optional recipient email + scheduled-send date + note, pays via the same Checkout flow, gets a branded code (+ optional Apple/Google Wallet pass). Manager 'Gift Cards' screen: look up code, see remaining balance, redeem an amount at the bar (QR scan), void/reissue. Dashboard shows issued vs redeemed vs outstanding-liability. Scheduled-send via an edge-fn cron. ~7%/yr revenue lift + instant cash float; a slice never redeemed.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) One-tap refunds + dispute desk in the manager app -> On any customer order / gift card / deposit, a 'Refund' action (full or partial) calling Stripe Refunds API via edge fn, writing the refund back with reason + who issued it. A small 'Payments' inbox surfaces Stripe disputes/chargebacks with the evidence Stripe needs. Refund/dispute totals on the dashboard. Guardrail: refunds over a threshold require a confirm + log. Ship this in the SAME release as checkout — a storefront without two-tap refunds becomes owner phone calls.
- (missing, tier3, impact5, M) Daily payments reconciliation card on the Dashboard -> A dashboard widget pulling Stripe Balance/Payouts/Reporting via edge fn: today's online revenue by stream (merch, gift cards issued vs redeemed, deposits, balances, tips) net of Stripe fees and refunds, plus outstanding gift-card liability and 'deposits expiring soon' (auth holds release ~7 days). Tap any number to drill into the orders behind it. Sits beside the existing Luna card; the every-shift 'here's the money' glance.
- (missing, tier3, impact2, M) Online tipping prompt (hospitality-tuned, separate ledger line) -> Optional tip step on merch/gift-card checkout and especially the event-balance payment page: presets 18/20/22% + custom + clear 'no tip' (no coercive 30-35% on a t-shirt). Tips post as a SEPARATE ledger line so payouts and tip-pooling stay compliant (gratuity != service charge). A 'leave a tip for the team' link the bartender can text after a great private event. Lower daily utility, nice staff-morale lever.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Luna finance co-pilot: read access + proactive money cadences over the bridge -> Give the bridge live READ access to customer_orders, payments, gift_cards, the deposit/balance fields on parties, and Stripe payout data, plus a KNOWLEDGE PACK (Oregon=no in-house sales tax / destination tax on shipped merch; deposit & no-show policy; gift-card terms). Proactive luna_insights cadences: morning payout-reconciliation-by-stream briefing with fee/refund anomaly flags; alert when an auth-hold deposit is ~24h from auto-releasing (capture-or-release); 'pickup order paid 3h ago, still not marked ready' nudge; weekly outstanding-gift-card-liability + redemption-rate note; heads-up on refunds/disputes. DRAFT/ACT (write rows back, never auto-move money): drafts pickup-ready/shipping/gift-card-delivery emails and 'your event balance is due' messages with the Payment Link; pre-stages a refund with a reason for Bradley to approve.

### Reviews & Reputation
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Reviews data model (reviews + review_sources tables) + Luna read access -> New Supabase tables: review_sources (platform, external_business_id, aggregate_rating, review_count, last_synced) and reviews (source, external_id, author, rating, text, posted_at, reply_status, reply_text, theme_tags[], sentiment, area, featured bool). RLS + realtime enabled. luna_bridge role granted read on both ALONGSIDE its existing events/parties/messages reads — this cross-table read is the moat (correlate a slow-Friday review with the sold-out DJ night + 80-top upstairs).
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Unified Reputation Inbox route (/reputation) — GBP + Yelp + TripAdvisor stream -> New /reputation route + useReviews hook mirroring useMessages (realtime postgres_changes subscription, unique-topic channel pattern from useLuna). Phone-friendly stream with filter chips (source / star / needs-reply), rolling per-platform rating+count header cards. Reads the reviews table populated by reviews-sync.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, XL) reviews-sync edge function (GBP ingest + one-tap reply post) -> reviews-sync edge fn: pulls Google Business Profile reviews via the Business Profile API (extend existing Google OAuth), normalizes to the reviews schema, upserts by external_id. Yelp/TripAdvisor via aggregator feed/scrape-partner as fast-follow. Posts GBP reply one-tap; Yelp/TripAdvisor return a deep link (their APIs forbid programmatic replies). Scheduled via the same cron/poll cadence pattern. GBP API access approval is the long-pole risk.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Luna AI review-response drafter (one-tap, context-aware, 3 tones) -> Each incoming review auto-triggers a luna_message ('draft reply' + review row ref); home-lab Luna reasons with full bar context (knows the band, the 86'd cocktail, the upstairs party) and writes 3 tone options (Warm / Crisp / Apologetic-with-fix) back. Inbox shows draft, inline edit, one-tap Post (GBP) or copy-to-clipboard deep-link (Yelp/TA). Knowledge-pack guardrails: never invent comps, never argue, escalate legal/health to human-only, never gate.
- (partial, tier1, impact5, M) Reputation alerts to Bradley's phone (low-star / trending-negative) -> Home-lab Luna fires kind='alert' insights instantly on any <=3-star review or low table-side score, payload carrying review text + pre-drafted reply + 'who was working' (from event/party context). Add a daily 'reputation pulse' line to the existing 07:00 briefing. Add browser/web-push for true behind-the-bar urgency (net-new push registration + service worker). In-app surface = S; the push channel adds effort.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Table-side feedback QR (FTC-safe, non-gating, per-area) + submit-feedback fn -> Per-area QR codes (bar/upstairs/downstairs/patio) open a 10-sec PUBLIC mobile form: star tap + theme chips + optional comment. submit-feedback edge fn writes a feedback table (incl. captured area). CRITICAL non-gating design: EVERY guest, any rating, sees the same screen with BOTH a public-Google-review button AND a private 'tell the manager' box — public button never hidden. Low scores additionally fire a realtime alert. Per-area capture turns vibes into 'fix the upstairs bar Fridays.'
- (missing, tier3, impact4, M) Theme-based sentiment tracking + trend analytics tab -> Luna tags every review/feedback row with sentiment (pos/neutral/neg) + theme(s) on ingest. /reputation analytics tab: rating trend line, review velocity (reviews/week vs healthy cadence), theme heat-grid (rising/falling), star-by-platform breakdown, drill-into-theme. Dedicated 'private events' sentiment slice cross-referenced to the parties table — ties reputation to the highest-margin revenue, unique to Iggy's two-floor model.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) NPS / post-event reputation loop for private parties -> Auto-send a short NPS/CSAT to the party contact a set window after the event (hook follow_up_date + send-party-email + marketing_opt_in). Promoters (9-10) get a one-tap NON-gated nudge to leave a Google/TA review; detractors (0-6) spawn a Todo + a Luna-drafted apology/recovery email. NPS trend lands on the analytics tab + Todos board. Private-event guests are the warmest reviewers a bar gets and nothing asks them today.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, S) Luna reputation knowledge pack + guardrails (voice, do/dont, anti-gating) -> Extend the bridge system prompt with Iggy's brand voice, a do/don't list for replies (never comp without approval, never argue, name specifics like the band/sunset view/espresso martini), escalate health/legal to human-only, and the hard anti-gating guardrail so Luna NEVER drafts anything that routes by sentiment (FTC up to $53,088/violation). Lives home-lab-side; lightweight but load-bearing for every Luna reputation feature.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) GBP & AI-search health + review-velocity optimizer (weekly audit) -> Reputation-tab scorecard: GBP completeness, photo freshness, post recency, reply rate, review recency, citation consistency (name/address/hours match across GBP/Yelp/TA/public site). Luna runs a weekly kind='suggestion' audit ('Hours conflict Yelp vs GBP'; '11 days since last GBP post'; 'reply rate 60%, push past 90%'). Velocity gauge nudges steady 90-day cadence over bursts. In 2026 these signals jointly drive Local-Pack rank AND whether AI search (AI Overviews/ChatGPT/Perplexity) cites Iggy's at all.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, M) Review-to-action bridge (Todos / Menu / Inventory / Specials) -> From any review/theme, 'turn into action': spin a Todo ('retrain upstairs bar on Friday pacing'), flag a menu/inventory item ('3 reviews call the espresso martini weak' -> link MenuManager/Inventory), or prefill a Specials/QuickPost leaning into a praised item ('guests love the sunset spritz'). Luna proactively suggests these in the briefing. Proves the app is one connected system, not bolted-on widgets.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, S) Public-site reviews & trust showcase (curated widget + schema.org) -> Curated, Luna-vetted reviews widget on Home + a Reviews/Testimonials section pulling best recent GBP/TA reviews with live aggregate rating + source badges. Bradley approves which surface via a 'feature on site' toggle in the reputation inbox (only real, published reviews). schema.org Review/AggregateRating markup earns rich-result stars in Google/AI results, lifting the BookEvent + Shop funnels. Lower daily utility, high board-wow + genuine revenue assist.

### Analytics, KPIs & Reporting
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Nightly Close-Out flow + daily_sales table (the data spine) -> A new `daily_sales` table (date, net_sales, covers/guest_count, labor_cost, optional bar/kitchen split, source enum manual|ocr|pos, weather_ref) plus a 20-second phone close-out form that pre-fills from the same-weekday last week. Optional photo-of-Z-report path reusing the Order Scanner edge-fn OCR. This unblocks ~80% of every real KPI (labor%, COGS%, prime cost, AOV, sales-per-cover).
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Manager Flash Report (benchmarked KPI strip replacing count-tiles) -> A phone-first KPI strip: Net Sales today vs same-weekday-last-week (▲/▼ %), Prime Cost %, Labor %, COGS %, Avg check / sales-per-cover, each with a green/amber/red traffic-light against 2026 bands (Prime 55-65%, COGS 28-32%, Labor <30%). Tap a tile → 30/90-day trend sparkline. Reads from daily_sales (+ inventory for COGS).
- (partial, tier1, impact5, M) Parties / Private-Events Revenue Dashboard (zero-POS, ships now) -> A dedicated analytics view: total private-event revenue by month (reuse computeInvoice across all confirmed parties), booked-vs-target pipeline, avg party value, revenue per space (upstairs/downstairs/whole), guest-count trends, inquiry→confirmed conversion %, lead-source breakdown (source field), lost revenue from cancellations, and confirmed forward revenue on the books for the next 90 days. Ships with NO POS dependency.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Weather-adjusted Slow-Night Detection (seaside differentiator) -> A weather-pull edge fn (daily temp/rain/wind for the Seaside OR location) joined to daily_sales history; flags when a night is forecast to underperform its weekday baseline AND attributes it: 'down -28% but it's 14°C and raining = weather, not a problem' vs 'down with no weather excuse = act on it.' Surfaces as a proactive Luna insight + a Calendar banner. This is the most defensibly-2026 feature for a seaside bar.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Event & Special ROI Scoreboard -> Per event-category: avg net sales on nights it ran vs matched baseline nights without it → a ranked 'lift per event type' list ('Trivia adds +$420/night avg; Karaoke is break-even'). Same for specials by active window. Turns the events program from gut-feel into a ranked ROI list — a board showstopper.
- (partial, tier1, impact4, M) COGS %, Waste-Cost & Inventory-Burn Analytics -> Waste Cost report ($ thrown away this month, by item — from waste-reason logs × cost_per_unit), days-of-cover burn-rate per item, and theoretical-vs-actual usage variance. Waste-cost and burn-rate ship immediately with zero new data. Once daily_sales exists, render true COGS% and pour-cost% (THE bar profitability metric). Surfaces biggest-money leaks, not just low-stock counts.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Boss-Grade Exportable PDF / Weekly Owner Pack (auto-emailed) -> One tap → branded report (KPI summary with traffic-lights, sales trend, prime-cost waterfall, top/bottom events by ROI, parties pipeline + confirmed forward revenue, waste-cost, 2-3 sentence narrative). Scheduled edge fn auto-emails the owner every Monday 8am via the existing Gmail integration; Luna writes the narrative. Highest boardWow artifact — recurring, unprompted, data-driven.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Simple 14-Day Sales Forecast + per-night staffing guide -> Lightweight forecast on Calendar/Dashboard projecting net sales for the next 14 days from same-weekday history + trend + events/parties booked that night + weather outlook, with a low/expected/high band. Each day shows a suggested staffing guide ('forecast $X → ~Y staff') so labor is scheduled to demand. ~10% accuracy gain ≈ ~3% revenue, up to 20% less waste.
- (partial, tier2, impact4, M) Luna analytics narration + proactive insight cadences -> Give the bridge live read access to daily_sales/sales_items + parties + inventory, plus a knowledge pack encoding 2026 benchmarks (Prime 55-65%, COGS 28-32%, Labor <30%) and Iggy's own rolling weekday baselines. Then: morning briefing insight, same-day weather-attributed slow-night alert with a suggested action, Friday pre-weekend forecast, and report-narrative drafting. Reuses the existing insight channel — net-new work is the cadence + knowledge pack on the home-lab side.
- (missing, tier3, impact5, L) POS Read-Only Sync (Square/Toast) edge function -> Optional pos-sync edge fn pulling daily net sales, item-level mix, voids/comps, and labor (if available) from Square/Toast Analytics API on a schedule into daily_sales + a new sales_items table — auto-populating every KPI with zero manual entry. Manual close-out remains the fallback. Architect daily_sales/sales_items now so this drops in later without a rebuild.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Menu / Item Profitability Matrix (menu engineering Stars/Dogs) -> Classic 2x2 (popularity × margin) classifying every item into Stars / Plowhorses / Puzzles / Dogs, crossing MenuManager prices with inventory cost for margin and sales mix for popularity; recommends re-pricing/repositioning/cutting. Bar-tuned cocktail pour-cost ranking. Highest-ROI analytics a sit-down bar can run and a very legible board visual.
- (missing, tier1, impact3, S) Charting / data-viz foundation (sparklines, trend lines, waterfall) -> A small, phone-first viz layer: lightweight SVG sparklines for KPI tiles, a sales trend line, an event-ROI bar ranking, and a prime-cost waterfall — either via a tree-shakeable lib (recharts ~ acceptable, or hand-rolled SVG to avoid bundle bloat on a phone-first app). Decide once, reuse across flash report, parties dashboard, and owner pack.

### CRM, Loyalty & Email/SMS
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Contacts/CRM route in the manager app (the missing front door) -> A /contacts route with a searchable, filterable, sortable list (name/email/phone/tags/source/last_visit/opt-in status) and a per-guest profile screen showing the full relationship timeline. This is the surface every other feature in this domain plugs into; nothing else is usable without it.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Unified Guest Profiles — promote contacts to a real guest graph -> Extend contacts with lifecycle fields (first_seen, last_visit, visit_count, total_spend, source) and a one-screen merged timeline that aggregates every touch the app already knows (parties, invoices, Messages/Gmail threads, future merch orders, loyalty activity). One deduped record per human.
- (partial, tier1, impact4, S) Dedupe contacts by email AND phone (fix findOrCreateContact) -> Match on normalized phone (E.164) OR email, with a merge path for already-split rows. Add a normalized phone column + index. This is a small surgical change but a hard prerequisite for a clean single guest view.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Frictionless walk-in capture (public QR signup + WiFi captive portal) -> A public mobile join page + submit-loyalty-signup edge function (mirroring submit-contact-message) behind table-tent QRs and a WiFi captive-portal redirect. Captures phone OR email + birthday month + explicit per-channel consent, writes to contacts with source tag (e.g. 'qr-downstairs', 'wifi-portal'). The single biggest list-growth lever; $0/msg and zero staff labor.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Consent & compliance ledger (per-channel opt-in audit + universal opt-out) -> Split into email_opt_in + sms_opt_in with a consent_events ledger (timestamp, source, IP/page, exact disclosure text). Honor opt-out by ANY method within 10 business days, plus a public opt-out form and a manager 'opt out' action from the inbox. HARD-block any send to a non-consented/opted-out contact at the send layer. Surface 10DLC registration status. Compliance-as-a-feature that prevents a single text becoming a TCPA suit.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Dynamic RFM segments (VIP / Regular / Occasional / Lapsed, auto-updating) -> A segment engine over unified profiles with built-in dynamic segments (VIP top-10% spend, Regular 3+/quarter, Occasional, Lapsed split Recently/Long/Dormant, Birthday-this-month, Event-only leads, Merch buyers) plus a simple rule builder. Recompute nightly via a scheduled edge function / Supabase cron. Each segment shows a live count and feeds the campaign composer.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Email + SMS campaign composer & scheduler (the blast tool) -> Pick a segment, write once, send to email and/or SMS, now or scheduled. New send-sms (Twilio/Telnyx, A2P-registered) and send-campaign (batched Resend/ESP) edge functions. Reuse SpecialEditor for the hero/SMS image and message_templates for merge fields. Pre-flight shows recipient count + est. SMS cost, auto-appends STOP/unsubscribe footers, throttles, and blocks non-consented numbers. The feature the owner pictures using on a slow Wednesday.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, L) Lifecycle automations (birthday, win-back, post-visit, welcome) -> Always-on triggered flows as scheduled edge functions reading the segment tables: Welcome (instant join reward), Birthday (fires on birthday month), tiered Win-Back (email then SMS to Recently/Long/Dormant lapsed), Post-Visit thank-you + review ask, Event-RSVP follow-ups. Each tracks sends/redemptions so the dashboard can show 'birthday flow recovered $X this month'. The highest-ROI, lowest-effort plays in hospitality — they run while Bradley tends bar.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Scheduling/cron infrastructure for marketing automations -> Supabase pg_cron (or a scheduled edge-function pattern) that runs nightly segment recompute and a marketing tick (fire due birthdays, win-back waves, scheduled campaigns, post-visit asks). A shared, idempotent dispatcher so automations and the composer's scheduled sends ride the same rail. Quiet-hours / send-window guard included.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, XL) Wallet-native digital loyalty (Apple/Google Wallet punch + points) -> A wallet loyalty pass (no app to install) issued from the QR signup — visual punch card and/or points, redeemable by the manager tapping 'redeem' on the guest profile or scanning the pass. New loyalty_* tables + a pass-generation edge function. Free $0/push lock-screen notifications to all pass-holders ('Trivia tonight, double points'). The most visibly modern feature on the board; XL because of the Apple developer + PassKit-style cert/signing setup.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Review engine (post-visit ask + smart routing to Google vs private inbox) -> A one-tap post-visit feedback request (via the post-visit automation) that routes happy guests to a public Google review and unhappy guests into a private message landing in the existing Inbox so Bradley fixes it before it goes public. Track review-link clicks + a running NPS-style score on the dashboard. Reputation flywheel that piggybacks on infra built anyway.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, M) Marketing dashboard & attribution (list growth + campaign ROI) -> A CRM/marketing tab on the existing Dashboard: total opted-in contacts + net weekly growth, growth by source (which QR / which event night), recent campaigns with open/click/redemption + est. revenue, loyalty redemptions, and consent health (opted-out %, unverified numbers). Per-campaign cost vs. est. covers driven so Bradley can prove marketing is a profit center — the single most credibility-building thing in the owner meeting.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, L) Luna as the marketing brain (knowledge pack + read access + draft-and-stage campaigns) -> Give the bridge an Iggy's marketing knowledge pack (segment defs, promos, event calendar, happy-hour windows, brand voice) + live READ access to contacts/loyalty/consent/campaign/parties tables. Proactive cadences to luna_insights (daily 'X guests went Lapsed, Y birthdays this month, Saturday RSVPs thin'; weather-aware patio-SMS nudges; post-campaign ROI reports). Draft-and-act: Luna pre-composes email+SMS copy into a draft campaign row, picks the segment, estimates cost/recipients, and stages it — Bradley taps approve. Critically, her staged send still hits the non-bypassable consent gate at the send layer, so autonomy never creates TCPA exposure.

### AI Ops Copilot Capabilities
- (missing, tier1, impact5, S) Iggy's Knowledge Pack + system prompt (versioned, glossary, mirrored to substrate) -> A versioned Iggy's brief the bridge loads into every prompt + mirrors into Luna's substrate: venue (upstairs/downstairs/whole rules, seaside seasonality, weather sensitivity), menu + happy-hour windows, event categories/cadence, package catalog + pricing logic (flat/per_person/per_hour), owner<->manager relationship, Bradley's email/social voice, house rules (gratuity_rate norms, confirmation timelines, follow-up cadence), plus a glossary mapping casual terms to actual tables/columns.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Ask-Your-Data: natural-language queries over every table -> Bridge reads the relevant Supabase tables (parties, packages, inventory_logs, events, menu costs, contacts), computes a grounded numeric answer, and replies via Realtime with a short answer + a 'sources' line. App renders that citation line under Luna bubbles so every number names the rows behind it.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, S) Grounded citations / 'sources' line under Luna answers and insights -> A small sources/refs convention on luna_messages and luna_insights.data (e.g. {sources:[{label,route,id}]}) that the bubble/card renders as tappable chips linking to the party/inventory/event row. Makes grounding visible and builds owner trust.
- (partial, tier1, impact5, S) Morning Briefing cadence (one push, one decision) -> A scheduled bridge job that writes a kind='briefing' row before open: last night recap, today's events+parties+space conflicts (Calendar logic already exists), this week's unconfirmed parties / follow-ups due (follow_up_date), low-stock items, and exactly ONE 'needs your call today' flag. Friday 'weekend-ahead' and Monday 'week-in-review' variants. Short enough to read at a red light.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Anomaly & risk alerts (proactive kind='alert' scans) -> Scheduled bridge scans firing an alert ONLY when something is off: item below par with no recent order_scan/restock; keg/spirit draining materially faster than trailing usage rate (over-pour/theft signal); space double-booked or a promoted Google Calendar event diverged from DB; inbox/Gmail thread unread > N hours; confirmed party past follow_up_date with no contact. Each alert carries a one-tap suggested action. Add a deposit_paid/deposit_amount field to parties to enable the deposit-chase scan.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Draft-and-approve action rows on insights (the 'act' half of the bridge) -> Bridge writes structured DRAFT rows (luna_drafts table OR a 'draft' status + payload on existing rows) that the app renders as a one-tap approve card: party follow-up email in Bradley's voice (into party_emails/send-party-email), supplier reorder from low-par items (into Order Scanner/inventory), event/happy-hour special caption handed to SpecialEditor draft, inbox auto-reply suggestion (send-reply). Nothing sends until Bradley taps approve — honoring the existing auto-reply guard and manual-share posture.
- (missing, tier2, impact3, S) Voice input for ask-your-data (phone-behind-the-bar) -> A mic button on the Luna composer using the Web Speech API (with graceful fallback) so Bradley can speak a question and get a grounded spoken-style answer back — no typing behind the bar. Pure client-side; rides the existing send pipeline.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Demand & staffing forecast (seaside + weather aware) -> A weekly kind='suggestion' forecast: projected busy-ness per day/shift from trailing event+party patterns, conditioned on weather forecast, weekday/weekend, scheduled event, holidays, and confirmed party headcounts. Plain output ('Saturday heavy — live music + 40-top downstairs + sunny 24C; staff up') and pairs forecast covers with inventory par to suggest pre-orders. Honest v1 without POS; clear upgrade path once sales data lands.
- (partial, tier2, impact4, M) Lead-scoring & pipeline nudges for private parties -> Bridge scores new inquiries by likelihood/value (guest_count, space, service type, urgency vs date), surfaces the highest-potential lead first, and writes nudge insights for stalled deals ('inquiry from 9 days ago, no follow_up — draft one?') and lapsed contacts ('office that booked last June hasn't rebooked — seasonal note?') using last_event_date + marketing_opt_in, respecting opt-in. Each nudge ends in a one-tap draft.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, M) Margin & menu-engineering copilot -> Luna computes per-item/per-cocktail margins from menu items/options + inventory cost-per-unit + supplier costs and surfaces them on demand and proactively ('lowest-margin cocktail is X; supplier cost +12% since April — re-price or swap garnish'). Flags happy-hour items below cost, suggests which specials to push for margin, and recommends mass-price-change candidates (feeds the existing bulk tool). Requires a lightweight recipe/ingredient mapping first.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, S) Conversational memory + owner-facing recap mode -> Bridge carries Iggy's context across days via the substrate (references prior flags) and adds an OWNER-mode recap Bradley can trigger or schedule: a clean monthly/quarterly narrative for the boss (party revenue, top events, inventory waste trend, what changed and why) generated from the same rows, in upward-presenting language. Pure board-wow, scales Bradley's reporting.

### Compliance, Safety & Maintenance
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Refusal & Cut-Off Log (dram-shop defense vault) -> One-thumb 'Refused / Cut Off' button on Dashboard -> guided 10-second form: auto-timestamp (server-set, non-backdatable), type (over-intoxication/underage/fake ID/aggressive), action_taken (refused sale / cut off / called cab-rideshare / called police), staff_id, optional discreet note + photo. New append-only table refusal_logs with edits captured as new rows (immutability enforced via RLS: insert-only for staff, no update/delete). A /compliance page lists/filters by date/staff/reason and exports a clean PDF packet for lawyer/insurer.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Incident Reports (slip-and-fall / injury / altercation) -> Guided incident form (facts-only with 'do not admit fault' coaching hint): type, location (upstairs/downstairs/deck/restroom), time, people, witnesses (name+contact), narrative, injuries, action taken, photos/video, emergency-services flag. Generates a numbered incident PDF; one-tap emails the packet via the existing Gmail multipart edge fn. Auto-links to the cleaning/floor-check log to pull 'floor last checked at X:XX, photo attached' as exculpatory evidence. New table incidents.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Live Temperature Wall + manual HACCP logging (Phase 1) -> Pick a unit (walk-in/beer cooler/reach-in/freezer/hot-hold) -> app shows safe range -> tap reading -> done. Out-of-range forces a corrective_action note (moved stock / called tech / discarded). Red/amber/green wall shows every unit's last check + who is overdue, surfaced on Dashboard and to Luna. New tables temp_units (with safe min/max) + temperature_logs (append-only, server timestamp).
- (missing, tier3, impact4, M) Wireless temperature sensor alerts (Phase 2) -> A tiny edge function webhook receives sensor pushes and writes temperature_logs rows every few minutes; the instant a walk-in drifts out of range it writes a luna_insights ALERT (and could be pushed to Bradley's phone overnight before a seafood walk-in spoils). Depends on Phase 1 schema; ship manual first, layer this second.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Opening / Closing & Weekly Safety Checklists (photo-proof, recurring) -> Role-aware recurring checklists run on the phone: Opening (unlock, lights, fridge temps logged, floors dry, POS up, first-aid stocked, exits clear), Closing (lock liquor, gas/equipment off, deposits secured, alarms set, last floor-check photo), Weekly Safety sweep (extinguisher tags, exit signs, deck railings/decking, GFI/electrical near water). Items can require a photo or a number (e.g. temp, which writes to temperature_logs). Each run is timestamped + signed by staff; misses flagged on Dashboard and surfaced to Luna. New tables checklist_templates, checklist_items, checklist_runs, checklist_responses.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, S) License / Permit / Insurance / Certification Tracker -> Single 'Credentials' page tracking liquor/business/food-service permits, music/PRS license, COI, fire inspection, plus per-staff RBS/server certs + food-handler cards. Each record: issuer, number, issue/expiry dates, document PDF/photo in Storage, renewal lead-time. Luna pushes escalating insights at 90/60/30/7 days; red banner when anything is expired. Staff-cert view shows current vs lapsed (joins to Team).
- (missing, tier3, impact4, XL) Cleaning & Preventive Maintenance Scheduler (QR assets + work orders) -> Recurring engine for cleaning (beer-line clean q2wk, glasswasher descale, deep-clean rota, restroom checks) + preventive maintenance of assets (walk-in, ice machine, glasswasher, HVAC filters monthly, deck/railing/exterior salt-air corrosion checks quarterly). Each asset gets a record + printable QR sticker -> scan on the unit to view history/log service. Failed check or breakage raises a Work Order (Critical/Standard/Scheduled, photo, assignee); owner sees an open-work-order board. New tables assets, maintenance_schedules, work_orders.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, S) Compliance Dashboard Score + Inspector-Ready Mode + Export Pack -> Single Dashboard tile: live Compliance Health score + traffic-light strip (temps logged today, opening checklist done, any license inside alert window, open critical work orders, incidents this week). Tap opens 'Inspector Mode' — clean full-screen read-only view of the temperature wall, today's checklists, current licenses/COI, last cleaning records, handable across the bar. One 'Export Compliance Pack (30/90 days)' button produces a single PDF binder for owner/insurer.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, S) Allergen Matrix & Supplier-Recall Register -> Add an allergen matrix (14 major allergens + contains-shellfish/nuts flags) per menu/food item so any staffer answers 'does this have nuts?' in two taps behind the bar. Add a supplier-recall log (product, lot, affected menu items, action taken pulled/discarded, date) producing a documented response trail — concretely relevant to a seaside oyster/shellfish bar's advisory exposure.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, S) Append-only / immutable evidence guarantee (cross-cutting legal foundation) -> Standardize all compliance log tables on: server-defaulted occurred_at + created_at (no client-supplied timestamps), RLS policies allowing INSERT but denying UPDATE/DELETE for staff, and edits modeled as new linked rows with an audit trail. Apply uniformly to refusal_logs, incidents, temperature_logs, checklist_responses, cleaning records, work_orders.

### Mobile-First Manager UX
- (missing, tier1, impact5, S) Installable PWA (Web App Manifest + service worker + home-screen install) -> Add vite-plugin-pwa (Workbox) to manager-app: generate manifest.webmanifest (name 'Iggy's Manager', short_name, standalone display, theme/background #0a0f0f, portrait, maskable 192/512 + an apple-touch-icon copied into manager-app/public), link it in index.html, and register a service worker. Result: Bradley taps Add to Home Screen once and Iggy's Manager opens full-screen with its own icon and splash, no browser chrome. This is the unlock that gates offline + push.
- (missing, tier1, impact3, S) App-update / version-freshness prompt (companion to the SW) -> With the SW in place, add Workbox skipWaiting+clientsClaim plus a 'New version available — tap to update' toast (reuse the existing react-hot-toast Toaster), and a small build-stamp/version string in Settings so Bradley can confirm what's live during the owner demo. Mandatory companion to caching: shipping a SW without this silently pins stale builds.
- (partial, tier1, impact3, S) Global haptics helper (app-wide buzz-on-confirm) -> Extract a tiny haptics util (e.g. haptic('tap'|'success'|'warn')) that wraps navigator.vibrate with named patterns, and call it on every commit app-wide: toggle special active, mark to-do done, send reply, confirm party, log inventory. Makes the whole app feel as physically confident as the Specials editor already does. Quick win that rides on patterns already proven in the codebase.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Offline-tolerant app shell + read cache (cooler-proof boot) -> Workbox app-shell precache (stale-while-revalidate for JS/CSS/icons) so the app boots instantly with zero network, plus a runtime cache of the read path Bradley actually pulls behind the bar (today's parties, today's events, low-stock items, recent messages, menu) into IndexedDB. Show a subtle 'Offline — showing last synced 6:12pm' banner instead of a blank error. Resync via Supabase Realtime on reconnect.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Command palette + global search (Cmd/Ctrl-K + mobile search pill) -> A cmdk-style palette that fuzzy-searches across all routes + records (party/contact/event/menu item by name) + actions (New event, Quick post, Mark all read, Add low-stock to order, Ask Luna). Desktop = Cmd-K; mobile = a persistent search pill atop the dashboard and/or long-press the FAB (phones have no key chord). Recent/frequent float to top. The 'Ask Luna…' entry writes a luna_messages row and streams the reply via Realtime. Reads as 'serious software' for the owner demo.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Web push notifications (booking / low-stock / Luna briefing) via VAPID Edge Function -> Add a push_subscriptions table + a VAPID Web Push Edge Function + a SW push/notificationclick handler. The home-lab Luna bridge (already writing luna_insights and reading parties/messages on a cadence) becomes the trigger source: 'New private-event inquiry from Sarah (Sat, 40)', 'Tito's below par — reorder?', '7am briefing ready', 'Party tonight 6pm downstairs, deposit unpaid.' Tapping deep-links to that party/message/insight. Permission requested contextually after first install, never on first load. The single most visceral 'this is a real app' demo moment.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Behind-the-bar gesture + speed layer (pull-to-refresh, swipe-actions, edge-back, big-tap density, prefetch/skeletons) -> Productize the existing primitives into a consistent system: pull-to-refresh on list pages; swipe-actions on rows (swipe message→archive, to-do→complete, party→call) reusing the BottomSheet drag mechanics; edge swipe-back; an optional one-handed/big-tap density mode pushing primary targets to 48-56px for wet thumbs; react-router prefetch + skeletons so nothing reads as a page load. Makes the whole app feel as native as the Specials editor.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Customizable quick-action launcher (long-press FAB radial + manifest home-screen shortcuts) -> Upgrade the FAB into a launcher: quick-tap still adds a party; long-press fans out a radial/sheet of Bradley's top 5-6 actions (Quick Post, New Event, Log Inventory, New To-Do, Ask Luna, Scan Order), reorderable and persisted in localStorage. Add manifest 'shortcuts' so a long-press on the installed home-screen icon jumps straight to Quick Post / New Party from outside the app. Turns the most-reachable pixel into a true launcher.
- (partial, tier3, impact4, L) Offline write queue / durable outbox (optimistic actions that sync on reconnect) -> An IndexedDB outbox: when offline/flaky, writes (toggle special, mark to-do done, adjust inventory, log waste, mark message read) commit optimistically to local state and queue, then flush to Supabase on reconnect (Background Sync where supported, foreground replay otherwise) using idempotency keys + upsert. A 'pending sync (3)' chip shows queue depth; conflicts surface a toast. Builds on the existing optimistic patterns rather than replacing them — the difference between tolerating offline reads and actually working offline.

### Events & Specials Automation Engine
- (partial, tier2, impact5, XL) One-Action Multi-Channel Publish (site + calendar + queued IG/FB post) -> A real Publish hub callable from an event OR a finished special. One tap fans out to: public site (active flag, already wired), Google Calendar (push, see two-way item), and a QUEUED social post written as a publish_jobs row. Per-channel copy auto-drafted (IG caption+hashtags, site blurb, calendar title) and editable before send. A publish-social edge function + the Luna bridge (or a Meta Graph long-lived token) does the actual hands-off post. Owner-side prereqs (IG Business acct + FB Page link + Meta App Review for instagram_content_publish) are documented in docs/SOCIAL-AND-SEO-SETUP.md and gate only the live auto-post — the queue/draft layer ships independently.
- (missing, tier1, impact5, M) Specials Auto-Expire & Auto-Schedule Window -> Add starts_at / expires_at (and optional daily time window) to specials. A special can be set to go live Fri 4pm and auto-retire Sun close; the public site only renders it inside the window (no manual toggle). A nightly pg_cron/edge sweep (or the Luna bridge) flips active off at expiry and optionally posts a last-call story. Specials list groups into Live now / Scheduled / Expired with a countdown chip. This is the cheapest honesty win and a hard dependency for scheduled social posts and playbooks.
- (partial, tier2, impact4, L) Recurring-Event Rules Engine (real RRULE + auto-instancing) -> Upgrade to a proper rule: weekly/biweekly/monthly, multi-day, date ranges, blackout dates, end date. Engine materializes next N concrete dated occurrences so the calendar, availability/conflict logic, public site, and social queue all see real instances. EventForm gets a Google-Calendar-style edit choice (this-and-future / just-this-one). This is the backbone the planner, conflict detection, and per-instance publishing stand on.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Happy-Hour Automation (live state + countdown, not a static page) -> Make happy hour a scheduled STATE: define the daily window once (e.g. Mon–Fri 3–6pm) in a small config/table. Public HappyHour page + Home banner auto-flip to 'Happy Hour ON — ends in 47 min' inside the window and 'Back tomorrow at 3' outside it, with holiday/blackout overrides. Optional auto-actions at window open (post an IG story via publish_jobs). High daily walk-up-conversion utility for a seaside crowd choosing between bars.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Themed-Night Playbooks (event rule + special + post template + prep checklist, self-assembling weekly) -> A playbook bundles: the event rule (e.g. Trivia every Wed), its linked auto-expiring special, a pre-built social-post template, a prep checklist with an owner spawned as todos, and a publish cadence (auto-post reminder 24h before). Launch once; every week it self-assembles. Most board-impressive feature — shows the system thinking like a programming director. Sits squarely on top of the rules engine, auto-expire, publish queue, and todos.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, L) Weekly Programming Planner (Mon–Sun board: events, specials, happy hour, parties on one grid) -> A single mobile-first, swipeable Mon–Sun board showing recurring nights, one-offs, live/scheduled specials, happy-hour windows, and parties on one grid. Drag to reschedule, tap a gap to drop in a playbook, conflicts flagged inline, and a 'Publish week' action that pushes the whole week's social queue at once. The Monday screen Bradley turns around to show the owner — makes the whole engine legible at a glance.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, L) Luna Programming Co-Pilot & Proactive Cadences -> Wire the engine into Luna as a domain expert: (a) give the bridge live READ on specials/happy_hour/publish_jobs + a durable KNOWLEDGE PACK (programming calendar, brand voice, sacred nights, HH rules); (b) proactive cadences beyond the briefing — empty-night watcher ('Wednesday's empty, drop in Trivia?'), expiry watcher ('Sunset special retires tonight — draft a last-call story?'), weather-tuned nudge ('78F sunny Saturday — push happy hour'); (c) DRAFT+QUEUE — Luna writes IG captions + queues posts as publish_jobs drafts, Bradley one-tap approves, trusted playbooks auto-go; (d) GUARDRAIL insights for stale specials, double-booked spaces, un-promoted Fridays, holiday-blackout misses. The differentiator no SaaS can copy.
- (partial, tier3, impact3, M) Two-Way Calendar Subscribe (public webcal/.ics feed + pull/push round-trip) -> A public webcal/.ics endpoint (edge fn) regulars subscribe to once — auto-updates when programming changes — plus genuine two-way sync: app events push to a public Google Calendar and Google edits flow back (close the loop the PromoteEventModal half-built), with recurring rules round-tripping. Turns the program into a self-updating retention loop on customers' phones.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, L) Post-Performance Loop (what actually filled seats) -> Tag each published event/special with lightweight outcome signals — social reach/saves from Meta Graph Insights, a rough sales-lift window (POS or a manual 'how'd it go' tap). The planner surfaces 'Trivia averages 40% above a normal Wednesday' / 'Espresso Martini special outperforms Frosé', and Luna uses it to recommend keep/kill/reschedule of playbooks. Depends on publish + playbook layers existing first; the feature that makes the engine compound across a season.

### Financials & Invoicing
- (missing, tier2, impact5, M) Invoice payment-lifecycle schema (deposit → balance → paid state on parties) -> Add persisted columns to `parties` (or a new `invoices` table keyed to party_id): invoice_number (sequential/branded), deposit_amount, amount_paid, balance_due (derived grandTotal - amount_paid), payment_status enum (unpaid|deposit_paid|paid_in_full), paid_at, deposit_due_date. Update the Party type + useParties hook to load them. This is the foundation every other money feature depends on.
- (missing, tier2, impact5, L) Stripe deposit + balance checkout (real hosted payment link, webhook-driven) -> New `create-checkout` edge function (Deno + Stripe secret key) that mints a hosted Payment Link / Checkout Session for a deposit (e.g. 25% or flat $200 room hold) or the balance due, tied to a party/invoice_id in metadata. A `stripe-webhook` edge function flips payment_status and stamps amount_paid/paid_at on checkout.session.completed. InvoicePanel gains 'Request deposit' / 'Send balance due' actions; the link goes out via the existing send-party-email path. Converts the events pipeline from quotes into tracked, collectible receivables and kills no-shows. This is the #1 gap and a top owner ask.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, S) Paid / owed badges on Parties list, Invoices page, and InvoicePanel -> Once lifecycle fields exist, render a live status chip (Unpaid / Deposit paid · $X owed / Paid in full) on each Invoices row and Party card, and replace the single grand-total with paid-vs-outstanding totals. Cheap render layer on top of the schema work — high visibility, makes the events business look professional.
- (partial, tier1, impact4, S) Tax line + service-charge vs. gratuity distinction on the invoice engine (2026-compliant) -> Add a configurable sales_tax_rate line (default 0% for Oregon, but a real editable line) and split the gratuity concept into 'gratuity (optional tip)' vs 'service_charge (mandatory)', flagging service charges as NON-qualified-tip income. Persist tax + service-charge breakdown on the invoice record and render it in buildInvoiceHtml/buildInvoiceText. Low effort, makes every downstream number (P&L, tip-out, W-2) correct.
- (partial, tier1, impact3, S) Branded PDF invoice with invoice number & payment terms -> Extend the existing HTML template to show invoice_number, issue date, deposit due date, amount paid / balance due, payment terms, the tax/service-charge lines, and (when Stripe is live) a 'Pay deposit' button/QR. Reuses the working renderer — mostly additive once the schema + tax lines land.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Luna Morning Money Briefing (daily proactive financial card) -> A scheduled home-lab Luna cadence that each morning writes a 'briefing' insight: yesterday's events revenue + receivables movement, deposits collected, balances now due, vendor bills due this week, money-in vs money-out snapshot. Tapping it deep-links to the relevant page. Add a weekly 'Sunday close' briefing. No new app schema beyond the lifecycle + vendor-bill fields it reads. Highest-wow, the CFO-in-your-pocket the owner says 'wow' to and Bradley reads every shift.
- (partial, tier2, impact5, M) Vendor-bill (accounts-payable) ledger on the Order Scanner -> Extend `orders` (or a `vendor_bills` table) with bill_total, due_date, paid (bool/date), and category (liquor/beer/food/supplies). Roll up into 'owed this week', per-vendor spend trend, and a 'bills due' dashboard list. Feeds the cost side of the P&L and lets Luna flag 'liquor spend up 22% vs 4-week avg' / 'Sysco bill due Friday'. Highest-leverage cost capture because the hard OCR part is already done.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, M) Event tip-out / gratuity reconciliation worksheet (FLSA + 2026 tip compliance) -> On event close, split collected gratuity across on-shift staff via configurable rules (e.g. bartenders 70% / barbacks 20% / lead 10%), with a HARD guardrail blocking managers/supervisors from the pool (FLSA, no exceptions). Output + store a per-person payout sheet; tag mandatory service charges as NON-tip income for the 2026 separate-qualified-tip W-2 requirement. Removes a real source of staff disputes and payroll/audit risk.
- (missing, tier2, impact4, S) Daily bar-sales top-line entry (manual quick-entry / CSV import) -> A `daily_sales` table + a 1-tap dashboard quick-entry (date, net sales, optional covers) and/or a CSV/POS import, honestly labeled as manual until a real POS integration exists. Unblocks the net-sales line in the Morning Briefing and the P&L-lite top line. Small, but it's the missing primitive for the whole money story.
- (missing, tier3, impact5, L) Owner P&L-lite + cash-flow snapshot dashboard with benchmark chips -> A single owner-facing screen assembling a lite P&L from owned data: revenue (events paid + balances due + daily bar sales), COGS (vendor-bill spend by category + inventory usage at cost_per_unit), gratuity/tips out, net cash position — benchmarked live against 2026 standards (prime cost 55-65%, pour cost 18-24%, beverage 15-30%) with color-coded on-target/over chips. The capstone screen the owner most wants for the boss meeting; depends on the deposit lifecycle, vendor-bill ledger, and a sales input all existing first.
- (missing, tier3, impact3, M) Event-pipeline budgeting & forecast (booked vs. target revenue, pacing) -> Set a monthly/quarterly events revenue target; track booked (confirmed grand totals) + weighted pipeline (inquiries × likelihood) against it with a pacing line ('June is 78% to target; 3 open inquiries worth $4.2k would close the gap'). Luna proactively nudges on stale high-value inquiries while the month is behind. Turns Bradley from order-taker into revenue manager; strong board narrative, lower daily utility.
- (missing, tier1, impact4, S) Luna financial knowledge pack (bar economics + 2026 benchmarks) -> A durable knowledge-pack/system-prompt for the bridge: Iggy's gratuity default, room rate, Oregon no-sales-tax, target prime cost 55-65% / pour cost 18-24% / beverage 15-30%, tip-out rules, FLSA manager exclusion, and the 2026 No-Tax-on-Tips service-charge distinction — plus live read access to the new invoice/vendor-bill/sales tables. Grounds every briefing, anomaly alert, and drafted reminder. Cheap, high-leverage, and the prerequisite that makes the Morning Briefing actually smart.
- (missing, tier3, impact4, M) Luna anomaly alerts + drafted money-action emails (balance-overdue / spend-spike / stale inquiry) -> Home-lab Luna cadences that raise 'alert' insights ('event balance $1,400 overdue 9 days', 'liquor spend up 22% vs 4-week avg', 'Sysco bill due Friday', 'two high-value inquiries with no follow-up, month behind target') AND draft the matching action — a balance-due reminder via send-party-email or a stale-inquiry follow-up — queued for Bradley's one-tap approval. Surfacing the number AND the next action is the 2026 differentiator over a POS that only reports.

## Competitor Teardowns

### Toast / Square for Restaurants (category leaders for POS-attached bar/restaurant operations)
- Daily Pulse / End-of-Day snapshot on the dashboard (Toast Now 'Sales Summary' + day-over-day compare): Toast's single most-used artifact is the Sales Summary with quick day-over-day and same-day-last-week comparison, delivered to the owner/manager's PHONE via Toast Now. It works because the value of ops data collapses to one question: 'Are we up or down vs normal, and why?' One glanceable card answers it. It is the feature owners check every single morning. (fit: Iggy's Dashboard already has a Luna card + insights feed — bolt a 'Today's Pulse' card next to it. Even without POS sales, Iggy's owns rich proprietary signal: events tonight, parties booked (parties table), low-stock count (inventory_items/par level), unread messages, pending todos, today's covers from confirmed parties, deposits due. Luna composes a one-line 'state of the bar' each morning as a luna_insight (kind=briefing) — 'DJ Night tonight + 1 upstairs party (40ppl, deposit unpaid), 3 items below par, 2 unread inquiries.' This is the highest-impact, lowest-cost steal and it makes the boss demo land in 5 seconds. Use the seaside angle: fold in a weather/tide line since foot traffic at a seaside bar is weather-driven — Luna can pull that into the briefing, which neither Toast nor Square does.)
- Manager Logbook / pre-shift notes (Toast 'manager log'): Toast surfaces an internal message board where managers post notes about guests, shifts, maintenance, VIPs, and operations — a running shift journal. It works because a bar's institutional memory otherwise lives in one person's head and walks out the door at shift change; the logbook is the cheapest possible knowledge-continuity tool and operators love it. (fit: Iggy's already has a todos table (owner<->manager board) — extend the same pattern into a lightweight, timestamped shift log (a 'notes' or 'shift_log' table, or a new todo type). Critical Iggy's-specific multiplier: the logbook becomes Luna's EYES into the floor. Because home-lab Luna reads all Supabase tables, every logbook entry ('regular Dave asked about the upstairs for his birthday', 'kegerator #2 leaking', 'ran out of limes at 9') becomes durable context Luna reasons over — feeding the knowledge pack, surfacing follow-ups, and connecting a floor note to a party inquiry or an inventory reorder automatically. Toast's logbook is dumb storage; Iggy's logbook is Luna's sensory input.)
- Behavioral guest CRM + automated 'we miss you' re-engagement (Toast Marketing/Loyalty/Guest Profiles): Toast's Regulars Report shows loyalty guests return at ~4x the rate of walk-ins, and 83-86% of email/SMS-driven orders come from people who already visited — i.e. the money is in re-engaging known guests, not acquiring strangers. Toast wins by attaching campaigns to real behavior (last visit, what they ordered, spend) so a 'we miss you' note actually converts. This is the single most ROI-positive feature the leaders bundle. (fit: Iggy's already has a contacts CRM (tags, marketing_opt_in, last_event_date) in the parties module — that is a behavioral CRM in embryo. Steal the re-engagement LOOP: Luna runs a cadence over contacts, finds opt-in parties hosts whose last_event_date is aging or whose follow_up_date has arrived, and DRAFTS a personalized 'we'd love to have you back for your next event' email (writing a row Bradley one-taps to send via the existing send-party-email edge fn). No new marketing platform, no $185/mo Toast Marketing bundle — it's the Luna bridge + an existing edge function. Adapt the trigger to a seaside event bar: re-engage past private-event hosts before their anniversary/birthday window, and segment by space (upstairs vs downstairs vs whole-venue) booked previously.)
- Labor-vs-sales / cost overlay surfaced in-the-moment (Toast Hourly Sales: labor as % of net sales in 15-min intervals; sales per labor hour): Toast's standout reporting move is overlaying COST on top of activity at the moment of decision — labor as a % of net sales by 15-min interval, sales-per-labor-hour, target band 25-35%. It works because a number is only actionable next to its benchmark; the overlay turns raw data into a staffing/cost decision a manager makes mid-shift. (fit: Iggy's doesn't have POS sales or a timeclock, so do NOT clone payroll/scheduling (that's the bloat trap). Steal the OVERLAY PRINCIPLE and apply it to the data Iggy's DOES own: inventory cost. Iggy's inventory_items already store cost/unit and supplier; inventory_logs track usage/waste/restock. Luna can compute and surface a 'cost overlay' — waste as a % of restock value, items burning above par-replenishment cadence, this week's spend-by-supplier vs last — and flag it proactively as a luna_insight. Tie it to events: 'Karaoke nights cost you 2x the lime/garnish waste of normal nights.' That's the seaside-bar version of Toast's labor overlay: cost intelligence on the data stream you actually control.)
- Commission-free online ordering + QR self-serve, and especially BOOKABLE online packages with prepay (Square Online self-serve ordering; Square customer prepayment / deposits): Square made online ordering and QR self-serve free and frictionless, and added customer PREPAYMENT for fixed-price bookings to protect cash flow and reduce no-shows. It works because every removed step (no app, no account, scan-and-pay; pay-deposit-to-confirm) directly lifts conversion and kills no-shows — proven mechanics, not speculation. (fit: This is owner ask #1 (group packages listed/bookable online) + ask #3 (real Stripe checkout for deposits) in one. Iggy's already has the packages catalog (flat/per_person/per_hour) and the BookEvent public page with an availability calendar — the missing link is exactly Square's prepay pattern: expose packages on the public site, let a host pick a package + space + date, and require a deposit via Stripe to CONVERT an inquiry into a confirmed party. Wire the existing-but-stubbed Stripe checkout to a new edge function, write the deposit/confirmation state back to the parties table (which already tracks deposit/confirmation fields and room_rate/hours). Square validates the whole funnel; Iggy's just needs to finish the plumbing it already scaffolded.)
- Gift cards as a first-class product (Square Gift Cards: physical + eGift): Square treats gift cards as a core, near-zero-effort revenue and acquisition channel — physical and digital, sold online and at point of sale. They work because they are pre-paid revenue (float), a guaranteed return visit, and a built-in referral (the recipient is a new guest), with almost no operational cost. (fit: Iggy's already has a Shop page with a cart and Stripe scaffolding (currently a stub) and Supabase Storage for assets. When the Stripe checkout gets wired for deposits/merch anyway, add a digital gift card SKU on the same rails — Iggy's Specials studio (canvas layers, gradients, GIF export) can even generate beautiful branded gift-card art with zero extra tooling. For a seaside destination bar, 'buy a gift card / experience voucher' is a natural tourist + local-gift purchase. It rides the exact same Stripe edge function as the deposit work, so marginal cost is low and it adds a real revenue line to show the boss.)
- Real two-way calendar sync as the system of record (Toast Tables / Square table & reservation management feeding one operational calendar): The leaders converge guest-facing bookings, reservations, and floor state into ONE operational calendar that staff trust as the single source of truth — no double-booking, no parallel paper book. It works because the calendar IS the operational nervous system of an events-driven venue; fragmentation across Google, paper, and the app is where revenue leaks. (fit: Owner ask #2 (real online calendar sync — subscribe/pull/push). Iggy's already has a unified Calendar (events + parties), availability/conflict logic, and Google Calendar integration via edge function — the gap is making the sync truly bidirectional and subscribable (publish an .ics the owner subscribes to; pull external holds back in). Steal Toast's 'one calendar is the source of truth' framing as the pitch. Iggy's upstairs/downstairs/whole-venue space model is actually MORE sophisticated than Square's generic floor plan for a two-space event bar — lean into that as a strength, and let Luna police conflicts proactively (it already reads events + parties), flagging an upstairs/downstairs double-book before it happens.)
- 86 / out-of-stock + low-stock as an active operational signal (Toast/Square KDS '86' an item, low-stock alerts): POS systems let staff '86' (mark unavailable) an item and propagate it instantly to menus and order screens, and trigger low-stock alerts. It works because a sold-out item shown to a guest is a broken promise and a refund; instant propagation protects the guest experience and the staff's sanity. (fit: Iggy's already has the hard parts: a low-stock widget, par levels, the Order Scanner (invoice photo -> line items -> match to inventory), and a DB-driven public menu (on_tap/off_tap/cocktails/etc.). Close the loop Toast-style: when an inventory_item tied to a menu item hits zero/below a threshold, let the manager (or Luna) flip a one-tap '86 / sold out' that propagates to the PUBLIC menu instantly — so the website never advertises a tapped-out beer. The on_tap/off_tap split is already perfect for this. Luna can even auto-suggest the 86 from inventory_logs and draft the reorder. This is a tiny build on top of existing tables with outsized guest-experience payoff.)
- Social/marketing publishing built into the ops loop (Toast/Square marketing tools that publish promos from inside the POS suite): The leaders keep promotion INSIDE the operations tool so a special or event becomes a marketing asset with one action, not a context-switch to a separate app. It works because the friction of leaving the tool is where most small-venue marketing dies. (fit: Owner ask #4 (true social AUTO-posting). Iggy's already has the BEST asset-creation surface here — the Specials canvas studio (layers, fonts, gradients, filters, GIF export, templates) is more capable than anything Toast/Square ship. Today it only opens the phone's manual share sheet. Steal the 'publish from inside the tool' principle and finish it: add real auto-posting via a publishing edge function (Instagram/Facebook Graph API), and let Luna schedule + draft the caption from event/special context, writing a queued post row Bradley approves. Tie it to the events table so 'Trivia Tuesday' auto-promotes on a cadence. This turns an already-impressive studio into an end-to-end create->schedule->publish pipeline — a strong boss-demo moment.)
- Proactive, scheduled operational alerts (Toast Now real-time alerts; Toast proactive reporting cadences): Beyond dashboards, the leaders PUSH — anomaly and threshold alerts (sales pace, labor over target, voids) so the manager doesn't have to remember to look. It works because attention is the scarce resource behind a bar; the tool earning trust is the one that interrupts only when it matters. (fit: This is Iggy's structural superpower and the cleanest fit of all: luna_insights are ALREADY a proactive push channel (kinds briefing/alert/suggestion; home-lab Luna writes them on a schedule). Steal Toast's CADENCE DISCIPLINE and define the right Iggy's triggers: morning briefing, deposit-unpaid-N-days-before-event alert, follow_up_date-due nudge, low-stock-before-a-busy-event warning, unanswered-inquiry-aging alert, contract/confirmation-not-signed flag. Iggy's already has the delivery mechanism Toast had to build from scratch — it just needs the cadence definitions in Luna's knowledge pack. This is the cheapest enterprise-grade capability on the list.)

### 7shifts / HotSchedules (Fourth) — restaurant workforce management: scheduling, labor compliance, tip pooling, team comms, engagement/retention
- Manager Logbook / Shift Log (the single highest-fit steal): HotSchedules/Fourth's Logbook is its stickiest feature: a per-day, per-shift running log of what happened — sales note, weather, VIPs in, equipment broke, a keg blew, who covered for whom — plus a 'Staff Journal' for one-line notes on individual employees. It survives manager turnover, makes handovers instant, and gives owners a searchable history. It works because a bar's institutional memory normally lives in one person's head; the logbook externalizes it cheaply. (fit: Perfect single-bar fit and trivial to build: one new shift_logs table (date, shift, author, free-text note, tags, optional photo to Storage) surfaced as a Dashboard card and a /log route. Bradley dictates a quick note from behind the bar at close. The killer Iggy's twist: Luna already polls the tables — feed shift_logs into the knowledge pack so Luna's morning briefing reads 'last 7 nights' logs', spots patterns ('3 of last 5 Fridays you ran out of the IPA on tap'), and ties notes to inventory_logs and events. That is something 7shifts/HotSchedules structurally cannot do because they have no agent with memory. Steal the form, supercharge with the bridge.)
- Post-shift feedback / team pulse (one-tap shift rating): 7shifts' Shift Feedback prompts staff with a quick post-shift rating + optional comment; managers see scores and trends and intervene before morale problems become turnover (they claim up to 13% turnover reduction). It works because it's a 5-second, low-friction emotional read on the floor that aggregates into a trend no single conversation reveals. (fit: High fit but must be RIGHT-SIZED for a tiny crew — do NOT build per-employee dashboards. Build a single shift_feedback table (shift date, optional staff name, 1-5 rating, free text) collected via a dead-simple link/QR staff tap at clock-out. The owner-facing value: a Dashboard trend sparkline + Luna proactively flagging dips ('Sat-night ratings dropped 3 weeks running, comments mention understaffing'). Pair it with Iggy's existing todos so Luna can auto-draft a follow-up. Keep it anonymous-optional so a 4-person crew still feels safe.)
- Real-time labor-vs-sales bar (daily, % of projected sales): The core of both platforms' manager value: a live gauge showing today's labor cost as a % of sales/projected sales, updating as the shift runs, so a manager throttles staffing in real time. It works because labor % is THE number a bar lives or dies on, and seeing it live changes same-day decisions (cut a shift early, call someone in). (fit: Adapt, don't over-engineer — Iggy's has no POS labor sync, so skip the forecasting engine. Build a simple daily-ops widget: manager taps in tonight's expected covers/sales and who's on, app shows projected labor % against a target Bradley sets. This is the single most owner-impressive number to put on the Dashboard because it directly speaks to Bradley's 'expanded responsibilities' (cost control). Later, Luna can learn typical sales per event-type from the events table and pre-fill the projection.)
- Custom tip-pooling rules + transparent payout sheet: 7shifts lets you define multiple pools with flexible contribution/distribution rules (FOH/BOH/hybrid, by hours or points), auto-calculates, and produces a payout record — saving ~8 hrs/week of manual math and, crucially, removing the #1 source of staff distrust (opaque tip splits). Transparency is the real product. (fit: Strong fit, scoped to ONE bar's rules. Don't build a rules engine — build Iggy's actual tip policy as a small calculator: enter total tips + who worked + hours/points, output each person's cut and save it to a tip_pools table for a permanent, disputable-proof record. Staff can view their own history. This is a concrete, visible win for the crew that costs little. Note 7shifts' on-demand 'Tip Payout' (pay tips to a debit card same-night via Clair) is a retention lever but OUT OF SCOPE — it needs banking rails; flag it as a 'someday' only.)
- Shout-Outs / public recognition: 7shifts' Shout-Outs is a public kudos feed for celebrating wins. Cheap dopamine that measurably lifts morale and retention; works because hospitality runs on recognition and a public feed makes it social. (fit: Very low-cost, high-warmth bolt-on, ideal for a small tight crew. Reuse Iggy's existing realtime + Luna insight pattern: a recognition entry (or a special luna_insights kind: 'shout_out') that shows on the Dashboard/Team page. Bradley taps a name + reason; everyone sees it. Luna can even prompt him ('Maya covered two shifts this week — want to shout her out?'). Tiny build, genuine engagement value to demo to the owner.)
- Shift Pool / self-serve swap board with manager approval: Both platforms' most-loved staff feature: employees post a shift they can't work, a coworker claims it, manager approves with one tap — eliminating the frantic 'can anyone cover Friday' group-text scramble. Works because it pushes coordination to the staff while keeping the manager in final control. (fit: Medium fit — only worth it IF Iggy's adds even a lightweight schedule (it currently has none; Team is just user management). If a basic shifts table exists, a swap-request flow (request → claim → Bradley one-tap approve, with realtime + push) replaces the text chaos and is exactly the kind of 'I manage the floor from my phone' feature that impresses the owner. If no schedule is built yet, treat this as Phase 2 behind a minimal schedule view. Avoid the full drag-and-drop builder.)
- Broadcast announcements with read receipts (SMS/push/email): 7shifts/HotSchedules let a manager blast one message to the whole team across channels and SEE who's read it — closing the 'I never saw that' loophole. Read receipts are the feature; the broadcast is table stakes. (fit: Good fit and Iggy's is already wired for it — Gmail edge functions + Supabase realtime exist. Build a one-to-many staff announcement (text + optional acknowledge button writing an 'ack' row so Bradley sees who's confirmed). Reuse message_templates.
